```usfm
\id PSA
\h Psalms
\toc1 Psalms
\mt1 Psalms
\ip GLE Render — Rahlfs-Hanhart LXX Greek base | Thayer (1889) + LSJ | Vulgate friction witness | v2.1 CAE block active | Greek-Catholic Psalter complete | LXX numbering throughout

\c 1
\p
\q1 Blessed is the man who has not walked in the counsel of the ungodly —
\q2 and has not stood in the way of sinners —
\q1 and has not sat in the seat of the pestilent.
\q1
\v 2 But his delight is in the law of the Lord —
\q2 and in his law he meditates day and night.
\q1
\v 3 And he will be as a tree planted beside the streams of water —
\q2 which will give its fruit in its season —
\q1 and its leaf will not wither —
\q2 and whatever he does will prosper.
\q1
\v 4 Not so the ungodly — not so —
\q2 but as the dust which the wind casts away from the face of the earth.
\q1
\v 5 Therefore the ungodly will not rise in judgment —
\q2 nor sinners in the council of the righteous.
\q1
\v 6 For the Lord knows the way of the righteous —
\q2 but the way of the ungodly will perish.

\c 2
\p
\q1 \v 1 Why did the nations rage —
\q2 and the peoples meditate empty things?
\q1
\v 2 The kings of the earth took their stand —
\q2 and the rulers were gathered together —
\q1 against the Lord and against his anointed.
\q1
\v 3 Let us break apart their bonds —
\q2 and cast away from us their yoke.
\q1
\v 4 The one who dwells in the heavens will laugh at them —
\q2 and the Lord will mock them.
\q1
\v 5 Then he will speak to them in his wrath —
\q2 and in his anger he will trouble them.
\q1
\v 6 But I was established as king by him —
\q2 upon Zion his holy mountain —
\q1
\v 7 declaring the ordinance of the Lord.
\q1 The Lord said to me: You are my son —
\q2 today I have begotten you.
\q1
\v 8 Ask of me and I will give the nations as your inheritance —
\q2 and the ends of the earth as your possession.
\q1
\v 9 You will shepherd them with a rod of iron —
\q2 and as a potter's vessel you will shatter them.
\q1
\v 10 And now O kings understand —
\q2 be instructed all you judges of the earth.
\q1
\v 11 Serve the Lord with fear —
\q2 and rejoice in him with trembling.
\q1
\v 12 Lay hold of instruction lest the Lord be angry —
\q2 and you perish from the righteous way.
\q1 When his wrath is kindled quickly —
\q2 blessed are all who trust in him.

\c 3
\d A Psalm of David when he fled from before Absalom his son.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord how they are multiplied who oppress me —
\q2 many are rising against me.
\q1
\v 2 Many say concerning my soul:
\q2 There is no salvation for him in his God.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 3 But you O Lord are my helper —
\q2 my glory and the one who lifts up my head.
\q1
\v 4 With my voice I cried to the Lord —
\q2 and he heard me from his holy mountain.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 5 I lay down and slept —
\q2 I awoke — for the Lord will help me.
\q1
\v 6 I will not fear ten thousands of people —
\q2 who set themselves against me round about.
\q1
\v 7 Arise O Lord — save me O my God —
\q2 for you have struck all my enemies on the cheek —
\q1 you have shattered the teeth of the ungodly.
\q1
\v 8 Salvation belongs to the Lord —
\q2 and your blessing is upon your people.

\c 4
\d For the end — a Psalm of David with songs.
\q1
\v 1 When I called — the God of my righteousness heard me.
\q2 In distress you gave me room —
\q1 have mercy on me and hear my prayer.
\q1
\v 2 O sons of men — until when will you be heavy of heart?
\q2 Why do you love vanity and seek falsehood?
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 3 And know that the Lord made wonderful his holy one —
\q2 the Lord will hear me when I cry to him.
\q1
\v 4 Be angry and do not sin —
\q2 what you say in your hearts — in your beds — be still.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 5 Sacrifice a sacrifice of righteousness —
\q2 and hope in the Lord.
\q1
\v 6 Many say: Who will show us good things?
\q2 The light of your face O Lord is sealed upon us.
\q1
\v 7 You gave gladness in my heart —
\q2 from the time of their grain and wine and oil they were multiplied.
\q1
\v 8 In peace I will both lie down and sleep —
\q2 for you O Lord alone caused me to dwell in hope.

\c 5
\d For the end — for the one who inherits — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Give ear to my words O Lord —
\q2 understand my cry.
\q1
\v 2 Attend to the voice of my supplication —
\q2 O my King and my God —
\q1 for to you I will pray.
\q1
\v 3 In the morning you will hear my voice —
\q2 in the morning I will stand before you and will watch.
\q1
\v 4 For you are not a God who wills iniquity —
\q2 the evil one will not dwell beside you.
\q1
\v 5 Nor will the lawless stand before your eyes —
\q2 you hate all who work iniquity.
\q1
\v 6 You will destroy all who speak falsehood —
\q2 the bloodthirsty and deceitful man the Lord abhors.
\q1
\v 7 But I — in the multitude of your mercy —
\q2 I will enter your house —
\q1 I will bow down toward your holy temple in fear of you.
\q1
\v 8 Lead me O Lord in your righteousness —
\q2 because of my enemies —
\q1 make your way straight before my face.
\q1
\v 9 For there is no truth in their mouth —
\q2 their heart is empty.
\q1 Their throat is an open tomb —
\q2 with their tongues they dealt deceitfully.
\q1
\v 10 Judge them O God —
\q2 let them fall from their own counsels.
\q1 According to the multitude of their impieties cast them out —
\q2 for they have provoked you O Lord.
\q1
\v 11 And let all who hope in you be glad —
\q2 they will rejoice forever and you will dwell among them.
\q1 And all who love your name will boast in you —
\q2
\v 12 for you will bless the righteous O Lord —
\q1 you have crowned us as with a shield of favor.

\c 6
\d For the end — among hymns — a Psalm of David for the eighth.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord do not rebuke me in your wrath —
\q2 and do not discipline me in your anger.
\q1
\v 2 Have mercy on me O Lord for I am weak —
\q2 heal me O Lord for my bones are troubled.
\q1
\v 3 And my soul is greatly troubled —
\q2 and you O Lord — until when?
\q1
\v 4 Turn O Lord — rescue my soul —
\q2 save me for the sake of your mercy.
\q1
\v 5 For in death there is no one who remembers you —
\q2 and in Hades who will confess to you?
\q1
\v 6 I am weary with my groaning —
\q2 I will wash my bed every night —
\q1 with my tears I will drench my couch.
\q1
\v 7 My eye is troubled by grief —
\q2 I grew old in the midst of all my enemies.
\q1
\v 8 Depart from me all you who work iniquity —
\q2 for the Lord has heard the sound of my weeping.
\q1
\v 9 The Lord has heard my supplication —
\q2 the Lord has received my prayer.
\q1
\v 10 Let all my enemies be put to shame and greatly troubled —
\q2 let them turn back and be put to shame very quickly.

\c 7
\d A Psalm of David which he sang to the Lord concerning the words of Cush the Benjaminite.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord my God — in you I hoped —
\q2 save me from all who pursue me and rescue me —
\q1
\v 2 lest like a lion he tear my soul —
\q2 while there is no one to ransom-release or to save.
\q1
\v 3 O Lord my God — if I did this —
\q2 if there is injustice in my hands —
\q1
\v 4 if I repaid those who repaid me evil —
\q2 let me then fall away empty before my enemies.
\q1
\v 5 Let the enemy pursue my soul and overtake it —
\q2 and trample my life upon the earth —
\q1 and bring my glory down to the dust.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 6 Arise O Lord in your wrath —
\q2 be exalted at the borders of my enemies —
\q1 and rise up O Lord my God —
\q2 according to the commandment which you commanded.
\q1
\v 7 And the gathering of peoples will surround you —
\q2 and return on high over it.
\q1
\v 8 The Lord will judge the peoples —
\q2 judge me O Lord according to my righteousness —
\q1 and according to my innocence upon me.
\q1
\v 9 Let the wickedness of sinners be brought to an end —
\q2 and you will establish the righteous —
\q1 O God who tests hearts and kidneys with righteousness.
\q1
\v 10 My help is from God —
\q2 who saves the upright in heart.
\q1
\v 11 God is a righteous judge —
\q2 and strong and patient —
\q1 not bringing wrath every day.
\q1
\v 12 If you do not turn — he will sharpen his sword —
\q2 he has bent his bow and made it ready.
\q1
\v 13 And on it he has prepared instruments of death —
\q2 he made his arrows for those who burn.
\q1
\v 14 Look — he travailed with injustice —
\q2 he conceived hardship and begot lawlessness.
\q1
\v 15 He dug a pit and hollowed it out —
\q2 and he will fall into the hole he made.
\q1
\v 16 His hardship will turn upon his own head —
\q2 and upon his crown his iniquity will come down.
\q1
\v 17 I will confess to the Lord according to his righteousness —
\q2 and I will sing to the name of the Lord Most High.

\c 8
\d For the end — concerning the winepresses — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord our Lord —
\q2 how wonderful is your name in all the earth —
\q1 for your majesty is lifted above the heavens.
\q1
\v 2 From the mouth of infants and nurslings you prepared praise —
\q2 because of your enemies —
\q1 to destroy the enemy and the avenger.
\q1
\v 3 For I will see your heavens — the works of your fingers —
\q2 the moon and the stars which you established.
\q1
\v 4 What is man that you remember him —
\q2 or the son of man that you visit him?
\q1
\v 5 You made him a little less than angels —
\q2 you crowned him with glory and honor.
\q1
\v 6 You set him over the works of your hands —
\q2 you subjected all things under his feet —
\q1
\v 7 all sheep and cattle —
\q2 and also the animals of the open-field —
\q1
\v 8 the birds of heaven and the fish of the sea —
\q2 the things passing through the paths of the seas.
\q1
\v 9 O Lord our Lord —
\q2 how wonderful is your name in all the earth.

\c 9
\d For the end — concerning the hidden things of the son — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 I will confess to you O Lord with my whole heart —
\q2 I will recount all your wonders.
\q1
\v 2 I will be glad and rejoice in you —
\q2 I will sing to your name O Most High.
\q1
\v 3 When my enemies turn back —
\q2 they will be weakened and perish from before your face.
\q1
\v 4 For you maintained my judgment and my cause —
\q2 you sat on the throne — the one who judges righteously.
\q1
\v 5 You rebuked the nations and the ungodly perished —
\q2 you blotted out their name forever and to ages of ages.
\q1
\v 6 The enemy — his swords have failed forever —
\q2 and you have destroyed their cities.
\q1 Their memory has perished with a sound —
\q2
\v 7 and the Lord endures forever.
\q1 He prepared his throne for judgment —
\q2
\v 8 and he himself will judge the world in righteousness —
\q1 he will judge peoples in uprightness.
\q1
\v 9 And the Lord became a refuge for the poor —
\q2 a helper in due time in affliction.
\q1
\v 10 And let those who know your name hope in you —
\q2 for you have not forsaken those who seek you O Lord.
\q1
\v 11 Sing to the Lord who dwells in Zion —
\q2 declare among the peoples his deeds.
\q1
\v 12 For he remembered — seeking their blood —
\q2 he did not forget the cry of the poor.
\q1
\v 13 Have mercy on me O Lord —
\q2 see my humiliation from my enemies —
\q1 you who lift me up from the gates of death —
\q2
\v 14 so that I may recount all your praises —
\q1 in the gates of the daughter of Zion —
\q2 I will rejoice in your salvation.
\q1
\v 15 The nations were mired in the corruption they made —
\q2 in the trap they hid their foot was caught.
\q1
\v 16 The Lord is known — he executes judgments —
\q2 the sinner was snared in the works of his hands.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 17 The sinners will be turned back to Hades —
\q2 all the nations who forget God.
\q1
\v 18 For the poor will not be forgotten forever —
\q2 the patience of the poor will not perish to the age.
\q1
\v 19 Arise O Lord — let not man prevail —
\q2 let the nations be judged before you.
\q1
\v 20 Appoint a lawgiver over them O Lord —
\q2 let the nations know that they are men.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 21 Why O Lord do you stand afar off —
\q2 overlooking in due times — in affliction?
\q1
\v 22 While the ungodly one shows pride the poor man is set on fire —
\q2 they are caught in the counsels which they devise.
\q1
\v 23 For the sinner is praised in the desires of his soul —
\q2 and the one who practices lawlessness is blessed.
\q1
\v 24 The sinner provoked the Lord —
\q2 according to the multitude of his wrath he will not seek.
\q1 God is not before him —
\q2 his ways are defiled at all times.
\q1
\v 25 Your judgments are taken away from before him —
\q2 he will master all his enemies.
\q1
\v 26 For he said in his heart: I will not be moved —
\q2 from generation to generation I will be without evil.
\q1
\v 27 His mouth is full of cursing and bitterness and deceit —
\q2 under his tongue are labor and pain.
\q1
\v 28 He sits in ambush with the rich in secret places —
\q2 to kill the innocent —
\q1 his eyes look away from the poor.
\q1
\v 29 He lies in wait in secret like a lion in his den —
\q2 he lies in wait to seize the poor —
\q1 to seize the poor by drawing him away.
\q1
\v 30 He will bring him low — he will bend and fall —
\q2 and he will master the poor by his mighty ones.
\q1
\v 31 For he said in his heart: God has forgotten —
\q2 he has turned away his face so as never to see.
\q1
\v 32 Arise O Lord God — let your hand be lifted up —
\q2 do not forget the poor.
\q1
\v 33 Why did the ungodly provoke God —
\q2 for he said in his heart: He will not seek.
\q1
\v 34 You see — for you observe labor and grief —
\q2 to deliver them into your hands.
\q1 The poor was left to you —
\q2 you became a helper to the orphan.
\q1
\v 35 Break the arm of the sinner and evil one —
\q2 his sin will be sought and will not be found.
\q1
\v 36 The Lord will reign as king forever and to ages of ages —
\q2 the nations will perish from his land.
\q1
\v 37 The desire of the poor you heard O Lord —
\q2 your ear attended to the preparation of their heart —
\q1 to judge for the orphan and the humble —
\q2 so that man may no longer boast upon the earth.

\c 10
\d For the end — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 In the Lord I hoped —
\q2 how will you say to my soul:
\q1 Flee to the mountains like a sparrow?
\q1
\v 2 For look — the sinners bent the bow —
\q2 they made ready their arrows in the quiver —
\q1 to shoot in the dark at the upright in heart.
\q1
\v 3 For what you established — they tore down —
\q2 but what has the righteous done?
\q1
\v 4 The Lord is in his holy temple —
\q2 the Lord — his throne is in heaven.
\q1 His eyes look upon the poor —
\q2 his eyelids examine the sons of men.
\q1
\v 5 The Lord examines the righteous and the ungodly —
\q2 but the one who loves injustice hates his own soul.
\q1
\v 6 He will rain snares upon sinners —
\q2 fire and brimstone and a wind of storm — their portion of the cup.
\q1
\v 7 For the Lord is righteous — he loves acts of righteousness —
\q2 his face sees uprightness.

\c 11
\d For the end — for the eighth — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Save me O Lord — for the holy one has failed —
\q2 for the faithful have diminished from among the sons of men.
\q1
\v 2 They spoke vanities each to his neighbor —
\q2 deceitful lips — they spoke with a double heart.
\q1
\v 3 The Lord will destroy all deceitful lips —
\q2 a tongue that speaks great things —
\q1
\v 4 those who said: We will magnify our tongue —
\q2 our lips are our own — who is lord over us?
\q1
\v 5 Because of the misery of the poor and the groaning of the needy —
\q2 now I will arise — says the Lord —
\q1 I will set in salvation — I will act openly in it.
\q1
\v 6 The words of the Lord are pure words —
\q2 silver tried by fire — tested for the earth — purified seven times.
\q1
\v 7 You O Lord will guard us —
\q2 and keep us from this generation forever.
\q1
\v 8 The ungodly walk in a circle —
\q2 according to your height you greatly despised the sons of men.

\c 12
\d For the end — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Until when O Lord will you forget me — to the end?
\q2 Until when will you turn your face from me?
\q1
\v 2 Until when will I place counsels in my soul —
\q2 grief in my heart by day?
\q1 Until when will my enemy be exalted over me?
\q1
\v 3 Look upon me and hear me O Lord my God —
\q2 give light to my eyes lest I sleep in death —
\q1
\v 4 lest my enemy say: I prevailed against him —
\q2 my oppressors will rejoice if I am shaken.
\q1
\v 5 But I hoped in your mercy —
\q2 my heart will rejoice in your salvation.
\q1
\v 6 I will sing to the Lord who did good to me —
\q2 and I will sing a psalm to the name of the Lord Most High.

\c 13
\d For the end — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 The fool said in his heart: There is no God.
\q2 They are corrupted and became abominable in their ways —
\q1 there is none who does good — not even one.
\q1
\v 2 The Lord looked down from heaven upon the sons of men —
\q2 to see if there is one who understands or seeks after God.
\q1
\v 3 All turned away — together they became useless —
\q2 there is none who does good — not even one.
\q1 Their throat is an open tomb —
\q2 with their tongues they dealt deceitfully —
\q1 the poison of asps is under their lips —
\q2 whose mouth is full of cursing and bitterness —
\q1 their feet are swift to shed blood —
\q2 destruction and misery are in their ways —
\q1 and the way of peace they have not known —
\q2 there is no fear of God before their eyes.
\q1
\v 4 Will not all who work iniquity know —
\q2 who devour my people as they eat bread —
\q1 they did not call upon the Lord?
\q1
\v 5 There they trembled with fear —
\q2 where there was no fear —
\q1 for God is in a righteous generation.
\q1
\v 6 You shamed the counsel of the poor —
\q2 because the Lord is his hope.
\q1
\v 7 Who will give from Zion the salvation of Israel?
\q2 When the Lord turns back the captivity of his people —
\q1 Jacob will rejoice and Israel will be glad.

\c 14
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord who will sojourn in your tent —
\q2 and who will dwell on your holy mountain?
\q1
\v 2 The one who walks blamelessly and works righteousness —
\q2 who speaks truth in his heart —
\q1
\v 3 who did not deal deceitfully with his tongue —
\q2 nor did evil to his neighbor —
\q1 nor took up a reproach against those near him.
\q1
\v 4 The evil one is brought to nothing in his sight —
\q2 but he glorifies those who fear the Lord.
\q1 The one who swears to his neighbor and does not break his oath —
\q2
\v 5 who did not give his money at interest —
\q1 nor take bribes against the innocent.
\q1 The one who does these things will never be shaken.

\c 15
\d A written inscription of David.
\q1
\v 1 Guard me O Lord — for in you I hoped.
\q2
\v 2 I said to the Lord: You are my Lord —
\q1 for you have no need of my goods.
\q1
\v 3 For the sake of the holy ones who are in his land —
\q2 he made all his will wonderful among them.
\q1
\v 4 Their troubles were multiplied —
\q2 after these things they hastened.
\q1 I will not gather their gatherings of blood —
\q2 nor will I remember their names with my lips.
\q1
\v 5 The Lord is the portion of my inheritance and my cup —
\q2 you are the one who restores my inheritance to me.
\q1
\v 6 The measuring-lines fell for me in excellent places —
\q2 yes my inheritance is excellent for me.
\q1
\v 7 I will bless the Lord who gave me understanding —
\q2 moreover my kidneys instructed me even in the nights.
\q1
\v 8 I set the Lord always before me —
\q2 for he is at my right hand so that I may not be shaken.
\q1
\v 9 Therefore my heart was glad and my tongue rejoiced —
\q2 moreover my flesh will dwell in hope.
\q1
\v 10 For you will not leave my soul in Hades —
\q2 nor will you give your holy one to see corruption.
\q1
\v 11 You made known to me the ways of life —
\q2 you will fill me with gladness with your face —
\q1 delight at your right hand to the end.

\c 16
\d A Prayer of David.
\q1
\v 1 Hear O Lord my righteousness —
\q2 attend to my petition —
\q1 give ear to my prayer not from deceitful lips.
\q1
\v 2 Let my judgment come from before your face —
\q2 let your eyes see uprightness.
\q1
\v 3 You tested my heart — you visited at night —
\q2 you tried me with fire and injustice was not found in me.
\q1 So that my mouth may not speak the works of men —
\q2
\v 4 by the words of your lips I kept hard ways.
\q1 Establish my steps in your paths —
\q2 so that my footsteps may not slip.
\q1
\v 5 I cried — for you heard me O God —
\q2 incline your ear to me and hear my words.
\q1
\v 6 Make your mercies wonderful — you who save those who hope in you —
\q2 from those who resist your right hand.
\q1
\v 7 Guard me as the apple of an eye —
\q2 in the shadow of your wings you will shelter me —
\q1
\v 8 from the face of the ungodly who troubled me —
\q2 my enemies who surrounded my soul.
\q1
\v 9 They enclosed their fatness —
\q2 their mouth spoke pride.
\q1
\v 10 Casting me out they now surrounded me —
\q2 they fixed their eyes to bow down upon the earth.
\q1
\v 11 They laid hold of me like a lion ready to prey —
\q2 and as a young lion dwelling in secret places.
\q1
\v 12 Arise O Lord — go before him and bring him low —
\q2 rescue my soul from the ungodly — your sword —
\q1
\v 13 from the enemies of your hand O Lord —
\q2 destroy them from the earth —
\q1 scatter them in their life.
\q1 And their belly was filled from your hidden things —
\q2 they were satisfied with sons —
\q1 and they left the remainder to their little ones.
\q1
\v 14 But I — in righteousness I will see your face —
\q2 I will be satisfied when your glory appears.

\c 17
\d For the end — a Psalm of David the servant of the Lord — who spoke to the Lord the words of this song on the day when the Lord rescued him from the hand of all his enemies and from the hand of Saul — and he said:
\q1
\v 1 I will love you O Lord my strength.
\q2
\v 2 The Lord is my firm support and my refuge and my deliverer —
\q1 my God is my helper and I will hope in him —
\q2 my protector and the horn of my salvation and my helper.
\q1
\v 3 Praising I will call upon the Lord —
\q2 and from my enemies I will be saved.
\q1
\v 4 The pangs of death surrounded me —
\q2 and torrents of lawlessness troubled me.
\q1
\v 5 The pangs of Hades surrounded me —
\q2 death's snares came before me.
\q1
\v 6 And in my affliction I called upon the Lord —
\q2 and to my God I cried.
\q1 He heard my voice from his holy temple —
\q2 and my cry before him came into his ears.
\q1
\v 7 And the earth was shaken and trembled —
\q2 and the foundations of the mountains were troubled and were shaken —
\q1 for God was wrathful with them.
\q1
\v 8 Smoke went up in his wrath —
\q2 and fire from his face devoured —
\q1 coals were kindled by it.
\q1
\v 9 And he bent heaven and came down —
\q2 and thick darkness was under his feet.
\q1
\v 10 And he mounted upon the cherubim and flew —
\q2 he flew upon the wings of winds.
\q1
\v 11 And he made darkness his hiding place —
\q2 around him his tent —
\q1 dark water in clouds of the air.
\q1
\v 12 From the brightness before him the clouds passed through —
\q2 hail and coals of fire.
\q1
\v 13 And the Lord thundered from heaven —
\q2 and the Most High gave his voice.
\q1
\v 14 He sent out his arrows and scattered them —
\q2 and he multiplied lightnings and threw them into confusion.
\q1
\v 15 And the springs of the waters were seen —
\q2 and the foundations of the world were uncovered —
\q1 at your rebuke O Lord —
\q2 at the blast of the breath of your wrath.
\q1
\v 16 He sent from on high and took me —
\q2 he drew me out of many waters.
\q1
\v 17 He will rescue me from my strong enemies —
\q2 and from those who hate me — for they are stronger than I.
\q1
\v 18 They came before me in the day of my affliction —
\q2 and the Lord became my support.
\q1
\v 19 And he brought me out into a broad place —
\q2 he rescued me — for he desired me.
\q1
\v 20 And the Lord will repay me according to my righteousness —
\q2 and according to the cleanness of my hands he will repay me.
\q1
\v 21 For I kept the ways of the Lord —
\q2 and did not act impiously away from my God.
\q1
\v 22 For all his judgments are before me —
\q2 and his ordinances I did not put away from me.
\q1
\v 23 And I will be blameless before him —
\q2 and I will keep myself from my lawlessness.
\q1
\v 24 And the Lord will repay me according to my righteousness —
\q2 and according to the cleanness of my hands before his eyes.
\q1
\v 25 With the holy one you will be holy —
\q2 and with the innocent man you will be innocent —
\q1 and with the chosen you will be chosen —
\q2 and with the crooked you will deal crookedly.
\q1
\v 26 For you will save the humble people —
\q2 and you will humble the eyes of the proud.
\q1
\v 27 For you will light my lamp O Lord my God —
\q2 you will illuminate my darkness.
\q1
\v 28 For in you I will be rescued from a raiding band —
\q2 and in my God I will leap over a wall.
\q1
\v 29 My God — his way is blameless —
\q2 the words of the Lord are tried with fire —
\q1 he is a protector to all who hope in him.
\q1
\v 30 For who is God except the Lord —
\q2 and who is God except our God?
\q1
\v 31 God who girds me with strength —
\q2 and made my way blameless.
\q1
\v 32 Who made my feet as deer's feet —
\q2 and sets me on the heights.
\q1
\v 33 Who trains my hands for battle —
\q2 and you made my arms as a bronze bow.
\q1
\v 34 And you gave me the shield of your salvation —
\q2 and your right hand supported me —
\q1 and your discipline set me upright to the end —
\q2 and your discipline itself will teach me.
\q1
\v 35 You broadened my step beneath me —
\q2 and my footsteps did not waver.
\q1
\v 36 I will pursue my enemies and overtake them —
\q2 and I will not turn back until they fail.
\q1
\v 37 I will crush them and they will not be able to stand —
\q2 they will fall under my feet.
\q1
\v 38 And you girded me with strength for battle —
\q2 you brought down under me those who rose against me.
\q1
\v 39 And you gave me the necks of my enemies —
\q2 and you destroyed those who hate me.
\q1
\v 40 They cried — and there was no savior —
\q2 to the Lord and he did not hear them.
\q1
\v 41 I will grind them as dust before the face of the wind —
\q2 as the mud of the streets I will blot them out.
\q1
\v 42 Rescue me from the contradictions of people —
\q2 you will set me at the head of nations —
\q1 a people whom I did not know served me —
\q2
\v 43 at the hearing of the ear they obeyed me —
\q1 sons of strangers lied to me.
\q1
\v 44 Sons of strangers grew old —
\q2 and they limped from their paths.
\q1
\v 45 The Lord lives and blessed is my helper —
\q2 and let the God of my salvation be exalted.
\q1
\v 46 O God who gives vengeance to me —
\q2 and brought peoples under me —
\q1
\v 47 my deliverer from angry enemies —
\q2 you will exalt me above those who rise up against me —
\q1 from the violent man you will rescue me.
\q1
\v 48 Therefore I will confess to you among the nations O Lord —
\q2 and I will sing a psalm to your name —
\q1
\v 49 giving great salvation to his king —
\q2 and showing mercy to his anointed —
\q1 to David and to his seed to the age.

\c 18
\d For the end — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 The heavens declare the glory of God —
\q2 and the firmament announces the work of his hands.
\q1
\v 2 Day to day utters speech —
\q2 and night to night announces knowledge.
\q1
\v 3 There are no speeches nor words —
\q2 whose voices are not heard.
\q1
\v 4 Their sound went out into all the earth —
\q2 and their words to the ends of the world.
\q1 In the sun he set his tent —
\q2
\v 5 and he himself comes out as a bridegroom from his chamber —
\q1 he will rejoice as a giant to run his course.
\q1
\v 6 His going forth is from the end of heaven —
\q2 and his circuit to its end —
\q1 and there is nothing hidden from his heat.
\q1
\v 7 The law of the Lord is blameless — converting souls —
\q2 the testimony of the Lord is faithful — giving wisdom to the little ones.
\q1
\v 8 The ordinances of the Lord are right — gladdening the heart —
\q2 the commandment of the Lord is radiant — enlightening eyes.
\q1
\v 9 The fear of the Lord is clean — enduring to the age of ages —
\q2 the judgments of the Lord are true — righteous altogether.
\q1
\v 10 More desirable than gold and much precious stone —
\q2 and sweeter than honey and the honeycomb.
\q1
\v 11 For your servant keeps them —
\q2 in keeping them there is great reward.
\q1
\v 12 Who understands transgressions?
\q2 From my hidden ones cleanse me —
\q1
\v 13 and from those of strangers spare your servant.
\q2 If they do not master me — then I will be blameless —
\q1 and I will be cleansed from great transgression.
\q1
\v 14 And the words of my mouth and the meditation of my heart —
\q2 will be acceptable before you always —
\q1 O Lord my helper and my redeemer.

\c 19
\d For the end — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 May the Lord hear you in the day of affliction —
\q2 may the name of the God of Jacob protect you.
\q1
\v 2 May he send you help from the sanctuary —
\q2 and support you from Zion.
\q1
\v 3 May he remember all your sacrifice —
\q2 and your whole-burnt offering may he accept.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 4 May he give you according to your heart —
\q2 and fulfill all your counsel.
\q1
\v 5 We will rejoice in your salvation —
\q2 and in the name of our God we will be magnified —
\q1 may the Lord fulfill all your requests.
\q1
\v 6 Now I know that the Lord saved his anointed —
\q2 he will hear him from his holy heaven —
\q1 in mighty acts the salvation of his right hand.
\q1
\v 7 These in chariots and these in horses —
\q2 but we will call upon the name of the Lord our God.
\q1
\v 8 They were bound and fell —
\q2 but we rose up and were made upright.
\q1
\v 9 O Lord save the king —
\q2 and hear us in the day when we call.

\c 20
\d For the end — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord in your strength the king will be glad —
\q2 and in your salvation how greatly will he rejoice.
\q1
\v 2 You gave him the desire of his heart —
\q2 and did not withhold the request of his lips.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 3 For you went before him with blessings of goodness —
\q2 you set upon his head a crown of precious stone.
\q1
\v 4 He asked life of you and you gave it to him —
\q2 length of days to the age and to the age of ages.
\q1
\v 5 Great is his glory in your salvation —
\q2 glory and great magnificence you will place upon him.
\q1
\v 6 For you will give him a blessing to the age of ages —
\q2 you will gladden him in joy with your face.
\q1
\v 7 For the king hopes in the Lord —
\q2 and in the mercy of the Most High he will not be shaken.
\q1
\v 8 Let your hand be found by all your enemies —
\q2 let your right hand find all who hate you.
\q1
\v 9 You will make them as a fiery oven in the time of your presence —
\q2 the Lord will trouble them in his wrath —
\q1 and fire will devour them.
\q1
\v 10 You will destroy their fruit from the earth —
\q2 and their seed from among the sons of men.
\q1
\v 11 For they stretched evil things against you —
\q2 they devised a counsel which they will not be able to establish.
\q1
\v 12 For you will set them as a back —
\q2 among your remnants you will prepare their face.
\q1
\v 13 Be exalted O Lord in your strength —
\q2 we will sing and praise your mighty acts.


```usfm
\c 21
\d For the end — a Psalm of David concerning the morning relief.
\q1
\v 1 O God my God — attend to me — why have you forsaken me?
\q2 Far from my salvation are the words of my transgressions.
\q1
\v 2 My God I will cry by day and you will not hear —
\q2 and by night and it is not foolishness for me.
\q1
\v 3 But you dwell in the holy place —
\q2 O praise of Israel.
\q1
\v 4 Our fathers hoped in you —
\q2 they hoped and you delivered them.
\q1
\v 5 To you they cried and were saved —
\q2 in you they hoped and were not put to shame.
\q1
\v 6 But I am a worm and not a man —
\q2 a reproach of men and despised of the people.
\q1
\v 7 All who saw me mocked me —
\q2 they spoke with their lips — they shook their heads.
\q1
\v 8 He hoped in the Lord — let him rescue him —
\q2 let him save him — for he delights in him.
\q1
\v 9 For you are the one who drew me from the womb —
\q2 my hope from my mother's breasts.
\q1
\v 10 Upon you I was cast from the womb —
\q2 from my mother's belly you are my God.
\q1
\v 11 Do not stand far from me —
\q2 for affliction is near — for there is no helper.
\q1
\v 12 Many bulls surrounded me —
\q2 fat bulls encircled me.
\q1
\v 13 They opened their mouth against me —
\q2 as a ravening and roaring lion.
\q1
\v 14 I was poured out as water —
\q2 and all my bones were scattered.
\q1 My heart became as melting wax —
\q2 in the midst of my belly.
\q1
\v 15 My strength dried up as a potsherd —
\q2 and my tongue cleaved to my throat —
\q1 and to the dust of death you brought me down.
\q1
\v 16 For many dogs surrounded me —
\q2 a gathering of evil ones encircled me —
\q1 they pierced my hands and my feet.
\q1
\v 17 They counted all my bones —
\q2 and they themselves looked and stared at me.
\q1
\v 18 They divided my garments among themselves —
\q2 and over my clothing they cast a lot.
\q1
\v 19 But you O Lord — do not stand far off —
\q2 O my help — hasten to my aid.
\q1
\v 20 Rescue my soul from the sword —
\q2 and my only one from the hand of the dog.
\q1
\v 21 Save me from the mouth of the lion —
\q2 and my lowliness from the horns of the unicorns.
\q1
\v 22 I will recount your name to my brothers —
\q2 in the midst of the gathering I will praise you.
\q1
\v 23 You who fear the Lord — praise him —
\q2 all the seed of Jacob — glorify him —
\q1 let all the seed of Israel fear him.
\q1
\v 24 For he did not despise or disdain the petition of the poor —
\q2 and did not turn his face from me —
\q1 and when I cried to him he heard me.
\q1
\v 25 From you comes my praise in the great gathering —
\q2 my vows I will pay before those who fear him.
\q1
\v 26 The poor will eat and be satisfied —
\q2 and those who seek him will praise the Lord —
\q1 their hearts will live to the age of ages.
\q1
\v 27 All the ends of the earth will remember and turn to the Lord —
\q2 and all the families of the nations will bow down before him.
\q1
\v 28 For the kingdom belongs to the Lord —
\q2 and he rules over the nations.
\q1
\v 29 All the fat ones of the earth ate and bowed down —
\q2 all who go down to the earth will fall before him —
\q1 and my soul lives for him.
\q1
\v 30 And my seed will serve him —
\q2 the coming generation will be declared to the Lord —
\q1
\v 31 and they will announce his righteousness —
\q2 to a people yet to be born — which the Lord made.

\c 22
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 The Lord shepherds me and I will lack nothing —
\q2
\v 2 in a place of green pasture there he made me dwell.
\q1 He nourished me beside water of rest —
\q2
\v 3 he restored my soul.
\q1 He led me in paths of righteousness —
\q2 for the sake of his name.
\q1
\v 4 Yes though I walk through the midst of the shadow of death —
\q2 I will not fear evils —
\q1 for you are with me —
\q2 your rod and your staff — they comforted me.
\q1
\v 5 You prepared a table before me —
\q2 in the presence of those who oppress me.
\q1 You anointed my head with oil —
\q2 and your cup fills me like the best wine.
\q1
\v 6 And your mercy will pursue me —
\q2 all the days of my life —
\q1 and I will dwell in the house of the Lord —
\q2 for length of days.

\c 23
\d A Psalm of David for the first day of the week.
\q1
\v 1 The earth is the Lord's and its fullness —
\q2 the world and all who dwell in it.
\q1
\v 2 He founded it upon the seas —
\q2 and upon the rivers he prepared it.
\q1
\v 3 Who will go up to the mountain of the Lord —
\q2 and who will stand in his holy place?
\q1
\v 4 The one of innocent hands and pure heart —
\q2 who did not take his soul in vain —
\q1 and did not swear deceitfully to his neighbor.
\q1
\v 5 This one will receive a blessing from the Lord —
\q2 and mercy from God his savior.
\q1
\v 6 This is the generation of those who seek him —
\q2 of those who seek the face of the God of Jacob.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 7 Lift up your gates O rulers —
\q2 and be lifted up you everlasting doors —
\q1 and the King of glory will enter.
\q1
\v 8 Who is this King of glory?
\q2 The Lord strong and mighty —
\q1 the Lord mighty in battle.
\q1
\v 9 Lift up your gates O rulers —
\q2 and be lifted up you everlasting doors —
\q1 and the King of glory will enter.
\q1
\v 10 Who is this King of glory?
\q2 The Lord of hosts — he is the King of glory.

\c 24
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 To you O Lord I lifted up my soul —
\q2
\v 2 my God in you I trusted — let me not be put to shame —
\q1 let not my enemies laugh at me.
\q1
\v 3 For all who wait for you will not be put to shame —
\q2 let those who act lawlessly without cause be put to shame.
\q1
\v 4 Lord make known to me your ways —
\q2 and teach me your paths.
\q1
\v 5 Lead me in your truth and teach me —
\q2 for you are the God of my salvation —
\q1 and I waited for you all the day.
\q1
\v 6 Remember your compassions O Lord —
\q2 and your mercies — for they are from the age.
\q1
\v 7 The sins of my youth and my ignorances — do not remember —
\q2 according to your mercy remember me —
\q1 for the sake of your goodness O Lord.
\q1
\v 8 Good and upright is the Lord —
\q2 therefore he will instruct sinners in the way.
\q1
\v 9 He will guide the gentle in judgment —
\q2 he will teach the gentle his ways.
\q1
\v 10 All the ways of the Lord are mercy and truth —
\q2 for those who seek his covenant and his testimonies.
\q1
\v 11 For the sake of your name O Lord —
\q2 you will be gracious to my sin — for it is great.
\q1
\v 12 Who is the man who fears the Lord?
\q2 He will instruct him in the way he chose.
\q1
\v 13 His soul will dwell in good things —
\q2 and his seed will inherit the earth.
\q1
\v 14 The Lord is the strength of those who fear him —
\q2 and his covenant is to make known to them.
\q1
\v 15 My eyes are always toward the Lord —
\q2 for he himself will draw my feet out of the snare.
\q1
\v 16 Look upon me and have mercy on me —
\q2 for I am alone and poor.
\q1
\v 17 The afflictions of my heart were multiplied —
\q2 bring me out of my necessities.
\q1
\v 18 See my humiliation and my labor —
\q2 and forgive all my sins.
\q1
\v 19 See my enemies — for they were multiplied —
\q2 and with unjust hatred they hated me.
\q1
\v 20 Guard my soul and rescue me —
\q2 let me not be put to shame — for I hoped in you.
\q1
\v 21 The innocent and the upright joined to me —
\q2 for I waited for you O Lord.
\q1
\v 22 O God ransom-release Israel —
\q2 from all his afflictions.

\c 25
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Judge me O Lord — for I walked in my innocence —
\q2 and hoping in the Lord I will not be weakened.
\q1
\v 2 Test me O Lord and try me —
\q2 purify my kidneys and my heart.
\q1
\v 3 For your mercy is before my eyes —
\q2 and I was well-pleased in your truth.
\q1
\v 4 I did not sit with a gathering of vanity —
\q2 and with those who act lawlessly I will not enter.
\q1
\v 5 I hated the gathering of evil ones —
\q2 and with the ungodly I will not sit.
\q1
\v 6 I will wash my hands in innocence —
\q2 and go around your altar O Lord —
\q1
\v 7 to hear the sound of praise —
\q2 and to recount all your wonders.
\q1
\v 8 O Lord I loved the beauty of your house —
\q2 and the place of the tabernacle of your glory.
\q1
\v 9 Do not destroy my soul together with the ungodly —
\q2 and my life with men of blood —
\q1
\v 10 in whose hands are lawless acts —
\q2 and their right hand is full of bribes.
\q1
\v 11 But I walked in my innocence —
\q2 ransom-release me and have mercy on me.
\q1
\v 12 My foot stood in uprightness —
\q2 in the gatherings I will bless you O Lord.

\c 26
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 The Lord is my light and my savior — whom will I fear?
\q2 The Lord is the defender of my life — of whom will I be afraid?
\q1
\v 2 When evil ones drew near upon me —
\q2 to eat my flesh —
\q1 those who oppress me and my enemies —
\q2 they themselves stumbled and fell.
\q1
\v 3 If an army encamps against me —
\q2 my heart will not fear.
\q1 If war rises against me —
\q2 in this I will hope.
\q1
\v 4 One thing I asked from the Lord — this I will seek —
\q2 to dwell in the house of the Lord all the days of my life —
\q1 to behold the delight of the Lord —
\q2 and to visit his holy temple.
\q1
\v 5 For he hid me in his tent in the day of evils —
\q2 he sheltered me in the hidden place of his tent —
\q1 upon a rock he lifted me up.
\q1
\v 6 And now he lifted up my head above my enemies —
\q2 I went around and sacrificed in his tent a sacrifice of rejoicing —
\q1 I will sing and make a psalm to the Lord.
\q1
\v 7 Hear O Lord my voice — I cried —
\q2 have mercy on me and hear me.
\q1
\v 8 To you my heart said: I sought your face —
\q2 your face O Lord I will seek.
\q1
\v 9 Do not turn your face from me —
\q2 and do not turn away in wrath from your servant.
\q1 Become my helper — do not cast me off —
\q2 and do not forsake me O God my savior.
\q1
\v 10 For my father and my mother forsook me —
\q2 but the Lord took me to himself.
\q1
\v 11 Set me in a law O Lord in your way —
\q2 and lead me in a straight path because of my enemies.
\q1
\v 12 Do not give me over to the souls of those who oppress me —
\q2 for false witnesses rose against me —
\q1 and injustice lied to itself.
\q1
\v 13 I believe to see the good things of the Lord —
\q2 in the land of the living.
\q1
\v 14 Wait for the Lord — be manly —
\q2 and let your heart be strong —
\q1 and wait for the Lord.

\c 27
\d A Psalm of David at the completion of the tabernacle.
\q1
\v 1 To you O Lord I cried — O my God do not be silent from me —
\q2 lest you be silent from me and I become like those going down to the pit.
\q1
\v 2 Hear the voice of my supplication when I pray to you —
\q2 when I lift up my hands toward your holy temple.
\q1
\v 3 Do not drag me away together with sinners —
\q2 and with those who work iniquity —
\q1 who speak peace with their neighbors —
\q2 but evil is in their hearts.
\q1
\v 4 Give to them according to their works —
\q2 and according to the wickedness of their deeds —
\q1 according to the works of their hands give to them —
\q2 return their recompense to them.
\q1
\v 5 For they did not understand the works of the Lord —
\q2 and the works of his hands —
\q1 you will tear them down and will not build them up.
\q1
\v 6 Blessed is the Lord — for he heard the voice of my supplication.
\q2
\v 7 The Lord is my helper and my protector —
\q1 in him my heart hoped and I was helped —
\q2 and my flesh flourished again —
\q1 and from my will I will confess to him.
\q1
\v 8 The Lord is the strength of his people —
\q2 and the defender of the salvations of his anointed.
\q1
\v 9 Save your people and bless your inheritance —
\q2 and shepherd them and lift them up to the age.

\c 28
\d A Psalm of David at the departure of the tabernacle.
\q1
\v 1 Bring to the Lord O sons of God —
\q2 bring to the Lord sons of rams —
\q1 bring to the Lord glory and honor —
\q2
\v 2 bring to the Lord the glory of his name —
\q1 bow down to the Lord in his holy court.
\q1
\v 3 The voice of the Lord is upon the waters —
\q2 the God of glory thundered —
\q1 the Lord is upon many waters.
\q1
\v 4 The voice of the Lord in power —
\q2 the voice of the Lord in magnificence.
\q1
\v 5 The voice of the Lord breaking cedars —
\q2 and the Lord will shatter the cedars of Lebanon.
\q1
\v 6 And he will break them as the calf of Lebanon —
\q2 and the beloved one as a young son of unicorns.
\q1
\v 7 The voice of the Lord divides a flame of fire —
\q2
\v 8 the voice of the Lord shaking the desolate-place —
\q1 the Lord will shake the desolate-place of Kadesh.
\q1
\v 9 The voice of the Lord preparing the deer —
\q2 and he will uncover the thickets —
\q1 and in his temple everyone says: Glory.
\q1
\v 10 The Lord will dwell upon the flood —
\q2 and the Lord will sit as king forever.
\q1
\v 11 The Lord will give strength to his people —
\q2 the Lord will bless his people with peace.

\c 29
\d A Psalm — a song at the dedication of the house — of David.
\q1
\v 1 I will exalt you O Lord for you lifted me up —
\q2 and did not let my enemies rejoice over me.
\q1
\v 2 O Lord my God I cried to you and you healed me —
\q2
\v 3 O Lord you led up my soul from Hades —
\q1 you saved me from those going down to the pit.
\q1
\v 4 Sing a psalm to the Lord all you his holy ones —
\q2 and give thanks at the memory of his holiness.
\q1
\v 5 For there is wrath in his anger —
\q2 and life in his will —
\q1 in the evening weeping will lodge —
\q2 and in the morning gladness.
\q1
\v 6 And I said in my prosperity: I will never be shaken.
\q2
\v 7 O Lord in your will you provided for my beauty strength —
\q1 but you turned your face from me and I was troubled.
\q1
\v 8 To you O Lord I will cry —
\q2 and to my God I will make supplication.
\q1
\v 9 What profit is there in my blood —
\q2 in my going down to corruption?
\q1 Will dust confess to you —
\q2 or will it announce your truth?
\q1
\v 10 The Lord heard and had mercy on me —
\q2 the Lord became my helper.
\q1
\v 11 You turned my mourning into dancing for me —
\q2 you tore off my sackcloth and girded me with gladness —
\q1
\v 12 so that my glory may sing a psalm to you and not grieve —
\q2 O Lord my God I will confess to you forever.

\c 30
\d For the end — a Psalm of David in an ecstasy.
\q1
\v 1 In you O Lord I hoped — let me not be put to shame forever —
\q2 in your righteousness deliver me and rescue me.
\q1
\v 2 Incline your ear to me — make haste to rescue me —
\q2 be to me a protecting God and a house of refuge to save me.
\q1
\v 3 For you are my strength and my refuge —
\q2 and for your name's sake you will lead me and nourish me.
\q1
\v 4 You will bring me out from the snare which they hid for me —
\q2 for you are my protector.
\q1
\v 5 Into your hands I will commit my breath-of-life —
\q2 you ransomed-released me O Lord God of truth.
\q1
\v 6 You hated those who keep vanities in vain —
\q2 but I hoped in the Lord.
\q1
\v 7 I will rejoice and be glad in your mercy —
\q2 for you saw my humiliation —
\q1 you saved my soul from necessities —
\q2
\v 8 and did not shut me up in the hands of the enemy —
\q1 you set my feet in a broad place.
\q1
\v 9 Have mercy on me O Lord — for I am troubled —
\q2 my eye was troubled with grief —
\q1 my soul and my belly.
\q1
\v 10 For my life wasted away in grief —
\q2 and my years in groaning.
\q1 My strength was weakened in poverty —
\q2 and my bones were troubled.
\q1
\v 11 I became a reproach above all my enemies —
\q2 and to my neighbors very greatly —
\q1 and a terror to my acquaintances —
\q2 those who saw me outside fled from me.
\q1
\v 12 I was forgotten as a dead man out of heart —
\q2 I became as a lost vessel.
\q1
\v 13 For I heard the reproach of many who dwell around —
\q2 when they gathered together against me —
\q1 they plotted to take my soul.
\q1
\v 14 But I — in you I hoped O Lord —
\q2 I said: You are my God.
\q1
\v 15 My times are in your hands —
\q2 rescue me from the hand of my enemies —
\q1 and from those who pursue me.
\q1
\v 16 Make your face shine upon your servant —
\q2 save me in your mercy.
\q1
\v 17 O Lord let me not be put to shame — for I called upon you —
\q2 let the ungodly be put to shame and brought down to Hades.
\q1
\v 18 Let the deceitful lips be made dumb —
\q2 that speak lawlessness against the righteous —
\q1 in pride and contempt.
\q1
\v 19 How great is the multitude of your goodness O Lord —
\q2 which you hid for those who fear you —
\q1 which you worked for those who hope in you —
\q2 before the sons of men.
\q1
\v 20 You will hide them in the hiding of your face —
\q2 from the disturbance of men —
\q1 you will shelter them in a tent —
\q2 from the contradiction of tongues.
\q1
\v 21 Blessed is the Lord — for he made his mercy wonderful —
\q2 in a fortified city.
\q1
\v 22 But I said in my ecstasy: I was cast away from before your eyes —
\q2 therefore you heard the voice of my prayer —
\q1 when I cried to you.
\q1
\v 23 Love the Lord all his holy ones —
\q2 for the Lord seeks out the faithful —
\q1 and repays abundantly those who act proudly.
\q1
\v 24 Be manly and let your heart be strong —
\q2 all you who hope in the Lord.

\c 31
\d Understanding of David — when Doeg the Edomite came and announced to Saul — and said to him: David came to the house of Abimelech.
\q1
\v 1 Blessed are those whose lawless deeds were forgiven —
\q2 and whose sins were covered.
\q1
\v 2 Blessed is the man to whom the Lord will not reckon sin —
\q2 and in whose mouth there is no deceit.
\q1
\v 3 For I was silent — my bones grew old —
\q2 from my crying out all the day.
\q1
\v 4 For day and night your hand was heavy upon me —
\q2 I was turned to misery when a thorn pierced me.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 5 I made known my sin to you —
\q2 and I did not hide my lawless deed.
\q1 I said: I will confess against myself my lawless deed to the Lord —
\q2 and you forgave the impiety of my sin.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 6 For this every holy one will pray to you in a fitting time —
\q2 yet surely in the flood of many waters —
\q1 they will not draw near to him.
\q1
\v 7 You are my refuge from the affliction that surrounds me —
\q2 my joy — ransom-release me from those who surround me.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 8 I will give you understanding and instruct you in this way in which you will go —
\q2 I will fix my eyes upon you.
\q1
\v 9 Do not become as horse or mule — who have no understanding —
\q2 with bit and bridle their jaws are bound —
\q1 so that they do not draw near to you.
\q1
\v 10 Many are the whippings of the sinner —
\q2 but the one who hopes in the Lord — mercy will surround him.
\q1
\v 11 Be glad in the Lord and rejoice O righteous ones —
\q2 and boast all you upright in heart.

\c 32
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Rejoice in the Lord O righteous ones —
\q2 praise befits the upright.
\q1
\v 2 Give thanks to the Lord on the harp —
\q2 with the ten-stringed psaltery sing a psalm to him.
\q1
\v 3 Sing to him a new song —
\q2 play skillfully with a joyful sound.
\q1
\v 4 For the word of the Lord is upright —
\q2 and all his works are in faithfulness.
\q1
\v 5 He loves mercy and judgment —
\q2 the earth is full of the mercy of the Lord.
\q1
\v 6 By the word of the Lord the heavens were established —
\q2 and by the breath-of-life of his mouth all their host.
\q1
\v 7 He gathers the waters of the sea as in a wineskin —
\q2 he sets the depths in storehouses.
\q1
\v 8 Let all the earth fear the Lord —
\q2 and let all the inhabitants of the world be shaken before him.
\q1
\v 9 For he spoke and they came to be —
\q2 he commanded and they were created.
\q1
\v 10 The Lord scatters the counsels of nations —
\q2 he sets aside the thoughts of peoples —
\q1 and sets aside the counsels of rulers.
\q1
\v 11 But the counsel of the Lord remains to the age —
\q2 the thoughts of his heart to generation and generation.
\q1
\v 12 Blessed is the nation whose God is the Lord —
\q2 the people whom he chose as his own inheritance.
\q1
\v 13 The Lord looked down from heaven —
\q2 he saw all the sons of men.
\q1
\v 14 From his prepared dwelling-place —
\q2 he looked upon all who dwell on the earth —
\q1
\v 15 who fashioned their hearts alone —
\q2 who understands all their works.
\q1
\v 16 A king is not saved by a great force —
\q2 and a giant will not be saved by the greatness of his strength.
\q1
\v 17 A horse is a vain thing for salvation —
\q2 and in the greatness of its strength it will not be saved.
\q1
\v 18 Look — the eyes of the Lord are upon those who fear him —
\q2 upon those who hope in his mercy —
\q1
\v 19 to rescue their souls from death —
\q2 and to nourish them in famine.
\q1
\v 20 Our soul waits for the Lord —
\q2 for he is our helper and protector.
\q1
\v 21 For in him our heart will be glad —
\q2 and in his holy name we hoped.
\q1
\v 22 Let your mercy O Lord be upon us —
\q2 according as we hoped in you.

\c 33
\d Of David — when he changed his appearance before Abimelech — and he drove him away — and he departed.
\q1
\v 1 I will bless the Lord at all times —
\q2 his praise will be continually in my mouth.
\q1
\v 2 In the Lord my soul will be praised —
\q2 let the gentle hear and rejoice.
\q1
\v 3 Magnify the Lord with me —
\q2 and let us exalt his name together.
\q1
\v 4 I sought the Lord and he heard me —
\q2 and from all my sojournings he delivered me.
\q1
\v 5 Draw near to him and be enlightened —
\q2 and your faces will not be put to shame.
\q1
\v 6 This poor one cried and the Lord heard him —
\q2 and from all his afflictions he saved him.
\q1
\v 7 The angel of the Lord will encamp around those who fear him —
\q2 and he will rescue them.
\q1
\v 8 Taste and see that the Lord is good —
\q2 blessed is the man who hopes in him.
\q1
\v 9 Fear the Lord all his holy ones —
\q2 for there is no want to those who fear him.
\q1
\v 10 The rich became poor and hungry —
\q2 but those who seek the Lord will not be lacking any good thing.
\q1
\v 11 Come children — hear me —
\q2 I will teach you the fear of the Lord.
\q1
\v 12 Who is the man who desires life —
\q2 loving to see good days?
\q1
\v 13 Keep your tongue from evil —
\q2 and your lips from speaking deceit.
\q1
\v 14 Turn from evil and do good —
\q2 seek peace and pursue it.
\q1
\v 15 The eyes of the Lord are upon the righteous —
\q2 and his ears toward their petition.
\q1
\v 16 But the face of the Lord is against those who do evil —
\q2 to destroy their memory from the earth.
\q1
\v 17 The righteous cried and the Lord heard them —
\q2 and from all their afflictions he delivered them.
\q1
\v 18 The Lord is near to those who are of a broken heart —
\q2 and he will save the humble in breath-of-life.
\q1
\v 19 Many are the afflictions of the righteous —
\q2 and from all of them the Lord will deliver him.
\q1
\v 20 He guards all his bones —
\q2 not one of them will be broken.
\q1
\v 21 The death of the sinner is evil —
\q2 and those who hate the righteous will be held guilty.
\q1
\v 22 The Lord will ransom-release the souls of his servants —
\q2 and none of those who hope in him will be held guilty.

\c 34
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Judge O Lord those who wrong me —
\q2 fight against those who fight against me.
\q1
\v 2 Take hold of shield and buckler —
\q2 and rise up to my help.
\q1
\v 3 Draw out a spear and close the way against those who pursue me —
\q2 say to my soul: I am your salvation.
\q1
\v 4 Let those who seek my soul be put to shame and confounded —
\q2 let those who devise evils against me be turned back and put to shame.
\q1
\v 5 Let them be as dust before the face of wind —
\q2 and the angel of the Lord afflicting them.
\q1
\v 6 Let their way be darkness and slipperiness —
\q2 and the angel of the Lord pursuing them.
\q1
\v 7 For without cause they hid their snare for me —
\q2 without cause they reproached my soul.
\q1
\v 8 Let a snare come upon him which he does not know —
\q2 and let the trap he hid catch him —
\q1 and let him fall in it.
\q1
\v 9 But my soul will rejoice in the Lord —
\q2 it will be glad in his salvation.
\q1
\v 10 All my bones will say: Lord who is like you —
\q2 rescuing the poor from the hand of those stronger than he —
\q1 and the poor and needy from those who rob him?
\q1
\v 11 Unjust witnesses rose up —
\q2 and questioned me about things I did not know.
\q1
\v 12 They repaid me evil for good —
\q2 and bereavement to my soul.
\q1
\v 13 But I — when they troubled me — I put on sackcloth —
\q2 and I humbled my soul with fasting —
\q1 and my prayer will return into my own bosom.
\q1
\v 14 I behaved as toward a neighbor and toward a brother of mine —
\q2 as one mourning and downcast — thus I was humbled.
\q1
\v 15 And against me they were glad and gathered together —
\q2 scourges were gathered against me and I did not know —
\q1 they were scattered and did not feel remorse.
\q1
\v 16 They tested me — they mocked me with mockery —
\q2 they gnashed their teeth at me.
\q1
\v 17 O Lord when will you look upon this —
\q2 restore my soul from their evil-doing —
\q1 my only one from the lions.
\q1
\v 18 I will confess to you in a great gathering —
\q2 in a strong people I will praise you.
\q1
\v 19 Let not those who are my enemies without cause rejoice over me —
\q2 those who hate me without cause and wink with eyes.
\q1
\v 20 For it is to those who are peaceful in the land that they speak —
\q2 and they devised deceitful things in their wrath.
\q1
\v 21 And they opened their mouth wide against me —
\q2 they said: Well done well done — our eyes saw.
\q1
\v 22 You saw O Lord — do not be silent —
\q2 O Lord do not stand far from me.
\q1
\v 23 Rouse yourself and attend to my judgment —
\q2 my God and my Lord — to my cause.
\q1
\v 24 Judge me O Lord my God according to your righteousness —
\q2 and let them not rejoice over me.
\q1
\v 25 Let them not say in their hearts: Well done our soul —
\q2 let them not say: We swallowed him up.
\q1
\v 26 Let those who rejoice in my evils be put to shame and confounded together —
\q2 let those who magnify themselves against me be clothed with shame and dishonor.
\q1
\v 27 But let those who desire my righteousness rejoice and be glad —
\q2 and let those who desire the peace of his servant say always:
\q1 Let the Lord be magnified.
\q1
\v 28 And my tongue will meditate your righteousness —
\q2 all the day your praise.

\c 35
\d For the servant of the Lord — of David.
\q1
\v 1 The transgression of the sinner speaks in him in his heart —
\q2 there is no fear of God before his eyes.
\q1
\v 2 For he acted deceitfully before him —
\q2 to find his lawless deed and to hate it.
\q1
\v 3 The words of his mouth are iniquity and deceit —
\q2 he did not desire to understand to do good.
\q1
\v 4 He devised iniquity on his bed —
\q2 he stood in every way that is not good —
\q1 and evil he did not abhor.
\q1
\v 5 O Lord your mercy is in the heaven —
\q2 and your truth reaches to the clouds.
\q1
\v 6 Your righteousness is as the mountains of God —
\q2 your judgments are a great deep.
\q1 Men and animals you will save O Lord —
\q2
\v 7 as your mercy was multiplied O God.
\q1 But the sons of men will hope in the covering of your wings —
\q2
\v 8 they will be drunk from the abundance of your house —
\q1 and you will give them to drink from the torrent of your delight.
\q1
\v 9 For with you is the fountain of life —
\q2 in your light we will see light.
\q1
\v 10 Extend your mercy to those who know you —
\q2 and your righteousness to the upright in heart.
\q1
\v 11 Let not the foot of pride come upon me —
\q2 and let not the hand of sinners shake me.
\q1
\v 12 There the workers of lawlessness fell —
\q2 they were thrust down and were not able to stand.

\c 36
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Do not be vexed because of evil-doers —
\q2 nor envy those who work lawlessness.
\q1
\v 2 For they will quickly wither as grass —
\q2 and as green herb they will quickly fall.
\q1
\v 3 Hope in the Lord and do good —
\q2 and dwell in the land and you will be shepherded by his truth.
\q1
\v 4 Take delight in the Lord —
\q2 and he will give you the requests of your heart.
\q1
\v 5 Reveal your way to the Lord and hope in him —
\q2 and he himself will act.
\q1
\v 6 And he will bring out your righteousness as light —
\q2 and your judgment as midday.
\q1
\v 7 Be subject to the Lord and implore him —
\q2 do not be vexed because of the one who prospers in his way —
\q1 at the man who does lawless things.
\q1
\v 8 Cease from wrath and forsake rage —
\q2 do not be vexed — so as to do evil.
\q1
\v 9 For evil-doers will be utterly destroyed —
\q2 but those who wait for the Lord — they will inherit the earth.
\q1
\v 10 And yet a little and the sinner will be no more —
\q2 and you will seek his place and not find it.
\q1
\v 11 But the gentle will inherit the earth —
\q2 and will delight in the multitude of peace.
\q1
\v 12 The sinner will watch for the righteous —
\q2 and will gnash his teeth against him.
\q1
\v 13 But the Lord will laugh at him —
\q2 for he foresees that his day is coming.
\q1
\v 14 Sinners drew out a sword —
\q2 they bent their bow —
\q1 to bring down the poor and the needy —
\q2 to slay those upright in the way.
\q1
\v 15 Let their sword enter into their own heart —
\q2 and let their bows be broken.
\q1
\v 16 Better is the little of the righteous —
\q2 than the great abundance of sinners.
\q1
\v 17 For the arms of sinners will be broken —
\q2 but the Lord supports the righteous.
\q1
\v 18 The Lord knows the ways of the blameless —
\q2 and their inheritance will be to the age.
\q1
\v 19 They will not be put to shame in an evil time —
\q2 and in days of famine they will be satisfied.
\q1
\v 20 For the sinners will perish —
\q2 and the enemies of the Lord at the moment of their glorying —
\q1 and being honored — will vanish like smoke they vanished.
\q1
\v 21 The sinner borrows and will not pay back —
\q2 but the righteous shows mercy and gives.
\q1
\v 22 For those who bless him will inherit the earth —
\q2 and those who curse him will be utterly destroyed.
\q1
\v 23 The steps of a man are set in order by the Lord —
\q2 and he will desire his way greatly.
\q1
\v 24 When he falls he will not be dashed down —
\q2 for the Lord supports his hand.
\q1
\v 25 I was young — and also I grew old —
\q2 and I never saw a righteous man forsaken —
\q1 nor his seed seeking bread.
\q1
\v 26 All the day he shows mercy and lends —
\q2 and his seed will be for a blessing.
\q1
\v 27 Turn from evil and do good —
\q2 and dwell to the age of ages.
\q1
\v 28 For the Lord loves judgment —
\q2 and will not forsake his holy ones —
\q1 they will be guarded to the age.
\q1 But the lawless will be driven out —
\q2 and the seed of the ungodly will be destroyed.
\q1
\v 29 The righteous will inherit the earth —
\q2 and dwell upon it to the age of ages.
\q1
\v 30 The mouth of the righteous will meditate wisdom —
\q2 and his tongue will speak judgment.
\q1
\v 31 The law of his God is in his heart —
\q2 and his steps will not slip.
\q1
\v 32 The sinner watches for the righteous —
\q2 and seeks to kill him.
\q1
\v 33 But the Lord will not leave him in his hands —
\q2 nor condemn him when he is judged.
\q1
\v 34 Wait for the Lord and keep his way —
\q2 and he will exalt you to inherit the earth —
\q1 you will see when sinners are utterly destroyed.
\q1
\v 35 I saw the ungodly greatly exalted —
\q2 and lifted up as the cedars of Lebanon.
\q1
\v 36 And I passed by and look — he was not —
\q2 and I sought him and his place was not found.
\q1
\v 37 Guard innocence and look upon uprightness —
\q2 for there is a remnant for the peaceable man.
\q1
\v 38 But the lawless will be utterly destroyed together —
\q2 the remnants of the ungodly will be destroyed.
\q1
\v 39 But the salvation of the righteous is from the Lord —
\q2 and he is their defender in the time of affliction.
\q1
\v 40 And the Lord will help them and rescue them —
\q2 and deliver them from sinners and save them —
\q1 because they hoped in him.

\c 37
\d A Psalm of David for a reminder concerning the sabbath.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord do not rebuke me in your wrath —
\q2 and do not discipline me in your anger.
\q1
\v 2 For your arrows were fixed in me —
\q2 and you pressed down your hand upon me.
\q1
\v 3 There is no healing in my flesh from the face of your wrath —
\q2 there is no peace in my bones from the face of my sins.
\q1
\v 4 For my lawless deeds went over my head —
\q2 as a heavy burden they weighed heavy upon me.
\q1
\v 5 My wounds stank and were corrupted —
\q2 from the face of my foolishness.
\q1
\v 6 I was wretched and bowed down exceedingly —
\q2 all the day I went about mourning.
\q1
\v 7 For my loins were filled with illusions —
\q2 and there is no healing in my flesh.
\q1
\v 8 I was afflicted and humbled exceedingly —
\q2 I roared from the groaning of my heart.
\q1
\v 9 O Lord before you is all my desire —
\q2 and my groaning is not hidden from you.
\q1
\v 10 My heart was troubled — my strength forsook me —
\q2 and even the light of my eyes — it also is not with me.
\q1
\v 11 My friends and my neighbors drew near and stood against me —
\q2 and those near to me stood far off.
\q1
\v 12 And those who sought my soul did violence —
\q2 and those who sought my evils spoke vanities —
\q1 and meditated deceits all the day.
\q1
\v 13 But I as a deaf man did not hear —
\q2 and as a dumb man who does not open his mouth.
\q1
\v 14 And I became as a man who does not hear —
\q2 and in whose mouth there are no reproofs.
\q1
\v 15 For in you O Lord I hoped —
\q2 you will hear O Lord my God.
\q1
\v 16 For I said: Let them never rejoice over me —
\q2 when my foot slipped they spoke great things against me.
\q1
\v 17 For I am ready for whippings —
\q2 and my pain is before me continually.
\q1
\v 18 For I will declare my lawless deed —
\q2 and I will be anxious concerning my sin.
\q1
\v 19 But my enemies live and are stronger than I —
\q2 and those who hate me unjustly were multiplied.
\q1
\v 20 Those who repay evil for good spoke against me —
\q2 because I followed goodness.
\q1
\v 21 Do not forsake me O Lord —
\q2 my God do not stand far from me.
\q1
\v 22 Attend to my help —
\q2 O Lord of my salvation.

\c 38
\d For the end — for Jeduthun — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 I said: I will guard my ways —
\q2 so as not to sin with my tongue.
\q1 I set a guard to my mouth —
\q2 while the sinner stood before me.
\q1
\v 2 I was dumb and was humbled —
\q2 and was silent from good things —
\q1 and my grief was renewed.
\q1
\v 3 My heart grew hot within me —
\q2 and in my meditation fire will burn —
\q1 I spoke with my tongue.
\q1
\v 4 Make known to me O Lord my end —
\q2 and what is the number of my days —
\q1 that I may know what I lack.
\q1
\v 5 Look — you made my days as handbreadths —
\q2 and my span is as nothing before you —
\q1 yet indeed all is vanity — every man who lives.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 6 Yet indeed man passes as an image —
\q2 indeed in vain he is troubled —
\q1 he stores up and does not know for whom he will gather.
\q1
\v 7 And now what is my patient waiting?
\q2 Is it not the Lord?
\q1 And my substance is before you.
\q1
\v 8 From all my lawless deeds ransom-release me —
\q2 you gave me as a reproach to the senseless.
\q1
\v 9 I was dumb and did not open my mouth —
\q2 for you are the one who made me.
\q1
\v 10 Remove your whippings from me —
\q2 from the strength of your hand I perished.
\q1
\v 11 In rebukes for lawless deeds you disciplined man —
\q2 and you made his soul waste away as a spider —
\q1 yet indeed every man is vanity.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 12 Hear my prayer O Lord and my supplication —
\q2 give ear to my tears —
\q1 do not be silent —
\q2 for I am a sojourner with you and a pilgrim —
\q1 as all my fathers.
\q1
\v 13 Spare me so that I may be refreshed —
\q2 before I depart and am no more.

\c 39
\d For the end — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Waiting I waited for the Lord —
\q2 and he attended to me and heard my petition.
\q1
\v 2 And he led me up from a pit of misery and from mud of mire —
\q2 and he set my feet upon a rock —
\q1 and directed my steps.
\q1
\v 3 And he put into my mouth a new song —
\q2 a hymn to our God.
\q1 Many will see and fear —
\q2 and will hope in the Lord.
\q1
\v 4 Blessed is the man whose hope is the name of the Lord —
\q2 and who did not look toward vanities and false delusions.
\q1
\v 5 You made great your wonders O Lord my God —
\q2 and in your thoughts toward us there is none to be compared to you —
\q1 I declared and I spoke —
\q2 they were multiplied beyond number.
\q1
\v 6 Sacrifice and offering you did not desire —
\q2 but ears you prepared for me —
\q1 whole-burnt offering and sin-offering you did not request.
\q1
\v 7 Then I said: Look I come —
\q2 in the heading of the scroll it is written concerning me.
\q1
\v 8 I desired to do your will O my God —
\q2 and your law is in the midst of my belly.
\q1
\v 9 I proclaimed righteousness in the great gathering —
\q2 look — I will not seal my lips —
\q1 O Lord you yourself knew.
\q1
\v 10 I did not hide your righteousness in my heart —
\q2 your truth and your salvation I spoke —
\q1 I did not hide your mercy and your truth —
\q2 from the great gathering.
\q1
\v 11 But you O Lord — do not withhold your compassions from me —
\q2 your mercy and your truth always kept me.
\q1
\v 12 For evils surrounded me beyond number —
\q2 my lawless deeds overtook me and I was not able to see —
\q1 they were multiplied beyond the hairs of my head —
\q2 and my heart forsook me.
\q1
\v 13 Be pleased O Lord to rescue me —
\q2 O Lord attend to my help.
\q1
\v 14 Let those who seek my soul to take it away be put to shame and confounded together —
\q2 let those who desire my evils be turned back and put to shame.
\q1
\v 15 Let those who say to me: Well done well done —
\q2 receive their shame instantly.
\q1
\v 16 Let all who seek you be glad and rejoice in you —
\q2 and let those who love your salvation say always:
\q1 Let the Lord be magnified.
\q1
\v 17 But I am poor and needy —
\q2 the Lord will take thought for me.
\q1 You are my helper and my deliverer —
\q2 my God do not delay.

\c 40
\d For the end — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Blessed is the one who considers the poor and needy —
\q2 in an evil day the Lord will deliver him.
\q1
\v 2 The Lord will guard him and preserve him alive and bless him on the earth —
\q2 and not give him over to the soul of his enemies.
\q1
\v 3 The Lord will help him upon a bed of grief —
\q2 you turned all his bed in his sickness.
\q1
\v 4 I said: O Lord have mercy on me —
\q2 heal my soul for I sinned against you.
\q1
\v 5 My enemies spoke evil against me:
\q2 When will he die and his name perish?
\q1
\v 6 And if he came to see — he spoke vanity —
\q2 his heart gathered lawlessness to itself —
\q1 he went outside and spoke in like manner.
\q1
\v 7 All my enemies whispered against me —
\q2 against me they devised evils for me.
\q1
\v 8 They ordained a lawless word against me:
\q2 Now that he has lain down will he not rise again?
\q1
\v 9 For even the man of my peace in whom I trusted —
\q2 who ate my bread —
\q1 lifted up his heel against me.
\q1
\v 10 But you O Lord have mercy on me and raise me up —
\q2 and I will repay them.
\q1
\v 11 By this I know that you desired me —
\q2 because my enemy will not rejoice over me.
\q1
\v 12 But you helped me because of my innocence —
\q2 and established me before you forever.
\q1
\v 13 Blessed is the Lord the God of Israel —
\q2 from the age and to the age.
\q1 Amen — Amen.

```usfm
\c 41
\d For the end — understanding of the sons of Korah.
\q1
\v 1 As a deer longs for the springs of water —
\q2 so my soul longs for you O God.
\q1
\v 2 My soul thirsted for God — for the living God —
\q2 when will I come and appear before the face of God?
\q1
\v 3 My tears became bread for me by day and by night —
\q2 when they said to me daily: Where is your God?
\q1
\v 4 These things I remembered and poured out my soul upon myself —
\q2 for I will pass over into the place of the wonderful tent —
\q1 to the house of God —
\q2 with a voice of rejoicing and confession — the sound of feasting.
\q1
\v 5 Why are you cast down O my soul —
\q2 and why do you trouble me?
\q1 Hope in God — for I will confess to him —
\q2 the salvation of my face — my God.
\q1
\v 6 My soul was cast down within me —
\q2 therefore I will remember you from the land of Jordan —
\q1 and of Hermon — from the small mountain.
\q1
\v 7 Deep calls to deep at the voice of your cataracts —
\q2 all your heights and your waves passed over me.
\q1
\v 8 By day the Lord commanded his mercy —
\q2 and by night his song is with me —
\q1 a prayer to the God of my life.
\q1
\v 9 I will say to God: You are my helper —
\q2 why did you forget me?
\q1 Why do I go mourning —
\q2 while the enemy oppresses me?
\q1
\v 10 While my bones were being crushed —
\q2 those who oppress me reproached me —
\q1 in saying to me daily: Where is your God?
\q1
\v 11 Why are you cast down O my soul —
\q2 and why do you trouble me?
\q1 Hope in God — for I will confess to him —
\q2 the salvation of my face and my God.

\c 42
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Judge me O God and plead my cause —
\q2 against an unholy nation —
\q1 from the unjust and deceitful man rescue me.
\q1
\v 2 For you are God my strength —
\q2 why did you cast me off?
\q1 Why do I go mourning —
\q2 while the enemy oppresses me?
\q1
\v 3 Send out your light and your truth —
\q2 they themselves led me —
\q1 and brought me to your holy mountain —
\q2 and to your tabernacles.
\q1
\v 4 And I will go in to the altar of God —
\q2 to the God who gladdens my youth —
\q1 I will give thanks to you with the harp O God my God.
\q1
\v 5 Why are you cast down O my soul —
\q2 and why do you trouble me?
\q1 Hope in God — for I will confess to him —
\q2 the salvation of my face and my God.

\c 43
\d For the end — for the sons of Korah — understanding.
\q1
\v 1 O God we heard with our ears —
\q2 our fathers told us —
\q1 the work you worked in their days —
\q2 in the days of old.
\q1
\v 2 Your hand destroyed nations and you planted them —
\q2 you afflicted peoples and cast them out.
\q1
\v 3 For not by their own sword did they inherit the land —
\q2 and their own arm did not save them —
\q1 but your right hand and your arm and the light of your face —
\q2 because you were well-pleased in them.
\q1
\v 4 You yourself are my king and my God —
\q2 who commands the salvations of Jacob.
\q1
\v 5 In you we will gore our enemies —
\q2 and in your name we will treat with contempt those who rise against us.
\q1
\v 6 For I will not hope in my bow —
\q2 and my sword will not save me.
\q1
\v 7 For you saved us from those who oppress us —
\q2 and those who hate us you put to shame.
\q1
\v 8 In God we will be praised all the day —
\q2 and in your name we will give thanks to the age.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 9 But now you have cast off and put us to shame —
\q2 and you will not go out with our forces.
\q1
\v 10 You turned us back from those who oppress us —
\q2 and those who hate us plundered for themselves.
\q1
\v 11 You gave us as sheep for food —
\q2 and among the nations you scattered us.
\q1
\v 12 You sold your people for nothing —
\q2 and there was no great price in their exchange.
\q1
\v 13 You made us a reproach to our neighbors —
\q2 a mockery and a derision to those around us.
\q1
\v 14 You made us a byword among the nations —
\q2 a shaking of the head among the peoples.
\q1
\v 15 All the day my shame is before me —
\q2 and the confusion of my face covered me —
\q1
\v 16 from the voice of the one reproaching and reviling —
\q2 from the face of the enemy and the pursuer.
\q1
\v 17 All these things came upon us but we did not forget you —
\q2 and we did not act lawlessly against your covenant.
\q1
\v 18 And our heart did not turn back —
\q2 and you did not turn aside our steps from your way.
\q1
\v 19 For you humbled us in a place of affliction —
\q2 and the shadow of death covered us.
\q1
\v 20 If we forgot the name of our God —
\q2 and stretched out our hands to a strange god —
\q1
\v 21 did God not seek these things out?
\q2 For he knows the hidden things of the heart.
\q1
\v 22 For on account of you we are being killed all the day —
\q2 we were reckoned as sheep of slaughter.
\q1
\v 23 Rouse yourself — why do you sleep O Lord?
\q2 Arise and do not cast off to the end.
\q1
\v 24 Why do you turn away your face —
\q2 and forget our poverty and our affliction?
\q1
\v 25 For our soul was humbled down to the dust —
\q2 our belly cleaved to the earth.
\q1
\v 26 Arise O Lord — help us —
\q2 and ransom-release us for the sake of your name.

\c 44
\d For the end — for those who will be changed — for the sons of Korah — understanding — a song concerning the beloved.
\q1
\v 1 My heart bubbled up with a good word —
\q2 I speak my works to the king —
\q1 my tongue is the pen of a swift writer.
\q1
\v 2 You are most beautiful in comeliness above the sons of men —
\q2 grace was poured out upon your lips —
\q1 therefore God blessed you to the age.
\q1
\v 3 Gird your sword upon your thigh O mighty one —
\q2 in your beauty and in your comeliness.
\q1
\v 4 And draw and prosper and reign —
\q2 on account of truth and gentleness and righteousness —
\q1 and your right hand will lead you wonderfully.
\q1
\v 5 Your arrows are sharp O mighty one —
\q2 peoples will fall under you —
\q1 in the heart of the enemies of the king.
\q1
\v 6 Your throne O God is to the age of ages —
\q2 the scepter of uprightness is the scepter of your kingdom.
\q1
\v 7 You loved righteousness and hated lawlessness —
\q2 therefore God your God anointed you —
\q1 with the oil of gladness beyond your companions.
\q1
\v 8 Myrrh and stacte and cassia from your garments —
\q2 from the ivory houses — from which they gladdened you.
\q1
\v 9 Daughters of kings honored you —
\q2 the queen stood at your right hand —
\q1 in gold-woven garments arrayed in varied colors.
\q1
\v 10 Hear O daughter and see and incline your ear —
\q2 and forget your people and the house of your father.
\q1
\v 11 And the king desired your beauty —
\q2 for he himself is your lord — and bow down to him.
\q1
\v 12 And the daughter of Tyre with gifts —
\q2 the rich of the people will entreat your face.
\q1
\v 13 All the glory of the daughter of the king is within —
\q2 in gold-woven fringes she is clothed —
\q1 arrayed in varied colors.
\q1
\v 14 The virgins after her — her companions —
\q2 will be brought to the king.
\q1
\v 15 They will be brought with gladness and rejoicing —
\q2 they will be led into the temple of the king.
\q1
\v 16 Instead of your fathers sons were born to you —
\q2 you will appoint them as rulers over all the earth.
\q1
\v 17 I will make your name remembered in every generation and generation —
\q2 therefore peoples will confess to you to the age and to the age of ages.

\c 45
\d For the end — for the sons of Korah — concerning hidden things — a song.
\q1
\v 1 God is our refuge and strength —
\q2 a helper in afflictions found abundantly.
\q1
\v 2 Therefore we will not fear when the earth is shaken —
\q2 and the mountains are moved into the heart of the seas.
\q1
\v 3 Their waters roared and were troubled —
\q2 the mountains were troubled by his might.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 4 The river's streams gladden the city of God —
\q2 the Most High made holy his tabernacle.
\q1
\v 5 God is in her midst — she will not be shaken —
\q2 God will help her toward morning.
\q1
\v 6 Nations were troubled — kingdoms tottered —
\q2 he gave his voice — the earth was shaken.
\q1
\v 7 The Lord of hosts is with us —
\q2 our helper is the God of Jacob.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 8 Come and see the works of the Lord —
\q2 what wonders he set upon the earth —
\q1
\v 9 removing wars to the ends of the earth —
\q2 he will shatter the bow and break the weapon —
\q1 and the shields he will burn with fire.
\q1
\v 10 Be still and know that I am God —
\q2 I will be exalted among the nations —
\q1 I will be exalted in the earth.
\q1
\v 11 The Lord of hosts is with us —
\q2 our helper is the God of Jacob.

\c 46
\d For the end — for the sons of Korah — a Psalm.
\q1
\v 1 All you nations clap your hands —
\q2 shout to God with a voice of rejoicing.
\q1
\v 2 For the Lord Most High is fearful —
\q2 a great king over all the earth.
\q1
\v 3 He subdued peoples under us —
\q2 and nations under our feet.
\q1
\v 4 He chose out for us his inheritance —
\q2 the beauty of Jacob which he loved.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 5 God went up with a shout —
\q2 the Lord with a voice of a trumpet.
\q1
\v 6 Sing a psalm to God — sing a psalm —
\q2 sing a psalm to our king — sing a psalm.
\q1
\v 7 For God is the king of all the earth —
\q2 sing a psalm with understanding.
\q1
\v 8 God reigned over the nations —
\q2 God sat upon his holy throne.
\q1
\v 9 The rulers of peoples were gathered together —
\q2 with the God of Abraham —
\q1 for the mighty ones of God were greatly exalted upon the earth.

\c 47
\d A Psalm — a song for the sons of Korah — on the second day of the week.
\q1
\v 1 Great is the Lord and greatly to be praised —
\q2 in the city of our God — his holy mountain.
\q1
\v 2 Well-planted in the joy of the whole earth —
\q2 is Mount Zion — the sides of the north —
