\q2 from age and to age —
\q1 and all the people will say: Amen.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 107
\d A song — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 My heart is ready O God — my heart is ready —
\q2 I will sing and make a psalm in my glory.
\q1
\v 2 Arise O my glory — arise psaltery and harp —
\q2 I will arise early.
\q1
\v 3 I will confess to you among the peoples O Lord —
\q2 I will make a psalm to you among the nations.
\q1
\v 4 For your mercy is great above the heavens —
\q2 and your truth to the clouds.
\q1
\v 5 Be exalted above the heavens O God —
\q2 and above all the earth your glory —
\q1
\v 6 so that your beloved ones may be rescued —
\q2 save with your right hand and hear me.
\q1
\v 7 God spoke in his holy place —
\q2 I will rejoice and divide Shechem —
\q1 and the valley of tabernacles I will measure out.
\q1
\v 8 Gilead is mine and Manasseh is mine —
\q2 and Ephraim is the strength of my head —
\q1 Judah is my king.
\q1
\v 9 Moab is the pot of my hope —
\q2 over Edom I will cast my sandal —
\q1 the foreigners were subjected to me.
\q1
\v 10 Who will lead me into the fortified city —
\q2 or who will lead me as far as Edom?
\q1
\v 11 Will you not O God who rejected us —
\q2 and will you not O God go out with our forces?
\q1
\v 12 Give us help from affliction —
\q2 for vain is the salvation of man.
\q1
\v 13 In God we will do mighty things —
\q2 and he himself will bring to nothing those who oppress us.

\c 108
\d For the end — a Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 O God do not pass by my praise in silence —
\q2
\v 2 for the mouth of the sinner and the mouth of the deceitful one were opened against me —
\q1 they spoke against me with a deceitful tongue.
\q1
\v 3 And with words of hatred they surrounded me —
\q2 and fought against me without cause.
\q1
\v 4 Instead of loving me they slandered me —
\q2 but I prayed.
\q1
\v 5 And they set against me evil in return for good —
\q2 and hatred in return for my love.
\q1
\v 6 Set a sinner over him —
\q2 and let a devil stand at his right hand.
\q1
\v 7 When he is judged let him come out condemned —
\q2 and let his prayer become sin.
\q1
\v 8 Let his days be few —
\q2 and let another take his overseership.
\q1
\v 9 Let his sons be orphans —
\q2 and his wife a widow.
\q1
\v 10 Tottering let his sons wander and beg —
\q2 and let them be cast out of their dwellings.
\q1
\v 11 Let the creditor search out all that is his —
\q2 and let strangers plunder his labors.
\q1
\v 12 Let there be no helper for him —
\q2 nor let anyone have mercy on his orphans —
\q1
\v 13 let his sons be for destruction —
\q2 in one generation let his name be blotted out.
\q1
\v 14 Let the lawless deed of his fathers be remembered before the Lord —
\q2 and the sin of his mother not be blotted out.
\q1
\v 15 Let them be before the Lord always —
\q2 and let his memory be destroyed from the earth —
\q1
\v 16 because he did not remember to show mercy —
\q2 and he pursued the poor man and needy —
\q1 and the one pierced in heart — to kill him.
\q1
\v 17 And he loved cursing and it will come to him —
\q2 and he did not desire blessing and it will stand far from him.
\q1
\v 18 And he clothed himself with cursing as with a garment —
\q2 and it entered as water into his inward parts —
\q1 and as oil into his bones.
\q1
\v 19 Let it be to him as a garment which he wraps around himself —
\q2 and as a belt which he girds himself with always.
\q1
\v 20 This is the work of those who slander me before the Lord —
\q2 and those who speak evil against my soul.
\q1
\v 21 But you O Lord — O Lord — deal with me for the sake of your name —
\q2 for your mercy is good — rescue me.
\q1
\v 22 For I am poor and needy —
\q2 and my heart was troubled within me.
\q1
\v 23 As a shadow when it declines I was removed —
\q2 I was shaken off as locusts.
\q1
\v 24 My knees grew weak from fasting —
\q2 and my flesh was changed from oil.
\q1
\v 25 And I became a reproach to them —
\q2 they saw me — they shook their heads.
\q1
\v 26 Help me O Lord my God —
\q2 save me according to your mercy —
\q1
\v 27 and let them know that this is your hand —
\q2 and you O Lord did it.
\q1
\v 28 They will curse and you will bless —
\q2 let those who rise against me be put to shame —
\q1 but your servant will rejoice.
\q1
\v 29 Let those who slander me be clothed with shame —
\q2 and let them wrap themselves in their dishonor as in a double cloak.
\q1
\v 30 I will give thanks greatly to the Lord with my mouth —
\q2 and in the midst of many I will praise him.
\q1
\v 31 For he stands at the right hand of the poor —
\q2 to save from those who pursue my soul.

\c 109
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 The Lord said to my Lord: Sit at my right hand —
\q2 until I make your enemies a footstool for your feet.
\q1
\v 2 The Lord will send out from Zion the rod of your power —
\q2 rule in the midst of your enemies.
\q1
\v 3 With you is the beginning in the day of your power —
\q2 in the splendors of the holy ones —
\q1 from the womb before the morning star I begot you.
\q1
\v 4 The Lord swore and will not repent:
\q2 You are a priest to the age according to the order of Melchizedek.
\q1
\v 5 The Lord at your right hand —
\q2 shattered kings in the day of his wrath.
\q1
\v 6 He will judge among the nations —
\q2 he will fill with corpses —
\q1 he will shatter the heads over much of the earth.
\q1
\v 7 From the torrent in the way he will drink —
\q2 therefore he will lift up the head.

\c 110
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 I will confess to the Lord with my whole heart —
\q2 in the council of the upright and in the gathering.
\q1
\v 2 Great are the works of the Lord —
\q2 sought out into all his will.
\q1
\v 3 His work is confession and magnificence —
\q2 and his righteousness remains to the age of ages.
\q1
\v 4 He made a memorial of his wonders —
\q2 the Lord is compassionate and merciful.
\q1
\v 5 He gave food to those who fear him —
\q2 he will remember his covenant to the age.
\q1
\v 6 The power of his works he announced to his people —
\q2 to give them the inheritance of nations.
\q1
\v 7 The works of his hands are truth and judgment —
\q2 all his commandments are faithful —
\q1
\v 8 established to the age of ages —
\q2 made in truth and uprightness.
\q1
\v 9 He sent ransom-release to his people —
\q2 he commanded his covenant to the age —
\q1 holy and fearful is his name.
\q1
\v 10 The beginning of wisdom is the fear of the Lord —
\q2 good understanding to all who do it —
\q1 his praise remains to the age of ages.

\c 111
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Blessed is the man who fears the Lord —
\q2 he will delight greatly in his commandments.
\q1
\v 2 His seed will be mighty upon the earth —
\q2 the generation of the upright will be blessed.
\q1
\v 3 Glory and riches are in his house —
\q2 and his righteousness remains to the age of ages.
\q1
\v 4 A light arose in darkness for the upright —
\q2 the compassionate and merciful and righteous one.
\q1
\v 5 Good is the man who shows mercy and lends —
\q2 he will order his words in judgment —
\q1
\v 6 for he will not be shaken to the age —
\q2 the righteous one will be in everlasting remembrance.
\q1
\v 7 He will not be afraid of an evil report —
\q2 his heart is ready — hoping in the Lord.
\q1
\v 8 His heart is steadfast — he will not fear —
\q2 until he looks upon his enemies.
\q1
\v 9 He scattered — he gave to the poor —
\q2 his righteousness remains to the age of ages —
\q1 his horn will be exalted in glory.
\q1
\v 10 The sinner will see and be angered —
\q2 he will gnash his teeth and melt away —
\q1 the desire of sinners will perish.

\c 112
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Praise O servants of the Lord —
\q2 praise the name of the Lord.
\q1
\v 2 Let the name of the Lord be blessed —
\q2 from now and to the age.
\q1
\v 3 From the rising of the sun to its setting —
\q2 the name of the Lord is praised.
\q1
\v 4 The Lord is high above all the nations —
\q2 above the heavens his glory.
\q1
\v 5 Who is like the Lord our God —
\q2 who dwells in the heights —
\q1
\v 6 and looks down upon the lowly things —
\q2 in heaven and upon earth?
\q1
\v 7 Who raises up the poor from the ground —
\q2 and from the dunghill exalts the needy —
\q1
\v 8 to seat him with rulers —
\q2 with the rulers of his people.
\q1
\v 9 Who makes the barren woman dwell in a house —
\q2 a glad mother of sons.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 113
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 When Israel went out from Egypt —
\q2 the house of Jacob from a people of foreign tongue —
\q1
\v 2 Judah became his sanctuary —
\q2 Israel his dominion.
\q1
\v 3 The sea saw and fled —
\q2 the Jordan turned back.
\q1
\v 4 The mountains leaped as rams —
\q2 and the hills as lambs of flocks.
\q1
\v 5 What is it with you O sea that you fled —
\q2 and you O Jordan that you turned back —
\q1
\v 6 you mountains that you leaped as rams —
\q2 and you hills as lambs of flocks?
\q1
\v 7 Before the face of the Lord the earth was shaken —
\q2 before the face of the God of Jacob —
\q1
\v 8 who turned the rock into pools of water —
\q2 the flint into springs of water.
\q1
\v 9 Not to us O Lord — not to us —
\q2 but to your name give glory —
\q1 for the sake of your mercy and your truth —
\q2 lest the nations say: Where is their God?
\q1
\v 10 But our God is in heaven —
\q2 he did all things whatever he willed.
\q1
\v 11 The idols of the nations are silver and gold —
\q2 works of men's hands.
\q1
\v 12 They have a mouth and will not speak —
\q2 they have eyes and will not see —
\q1
\v 13 they have ears and will not hear —
\q2 they have nostrils and will not smell —
\q1
\v 14 they have hands and will not feel —
\q2 they have feet and will not walk —
\q1 they will not sound with their throat.
\q1
\v 15 Let those who make them become like them —
\q2 and all who trust in them.
\q1
\v 16 The house of Israel hoped in the Lord —
\q2 he is their helper and protector.
\q1
\v 17 The house of Aaron hoped in the Lord —
\q2 he is their helper and protector.
\q1
\v 18 Those who fear the Lord hoped in the Lord —
\q2 he is their helper and protector.
\q1
\v 19 The Lord remembered us and blessed us —
\q2 he blessed the house of Israel —
\q1 he blessed the house of Aaron —
\q2
\v 20 he blessed those who fear the Lord —
\q1 the small with the great.
\q1
\v 21 May the Lord add upon you —
\q2 upon you and upon your sons.
\q1
\v 22 Blessed are you by the Lord —
\q2 who made heaven and earth.
\q1
\v 23 The heaven of heaven belongs to the Lord —
\q2 but the earth he gave to the sons of men.
\q1
\v 24 The dead will not praise you O Lord —
\q2 nor all who go down to Hades —
\q1
\v 25 but we the living will bless the Lord —
\q2 from now and to the age.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 114
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 I loved — because the Lord will hear —
\q2 the voice of my supplication.
\q1
\v 2 For he inclined his ear to me —
\q2 and in my days I will call upon him.
\q1
\v 3 The pangs of death surrounded me —
\q2 and the dangers of Hades found me —
\q1 I found affliction and grief —
\q2
\v 4 and on the name of the Lord I called:
\q1 O Lord rescue my soul.
\q1
\v 5 Merciful and righteous is the Lord —
\q2 and our God has mercy.
\q1
\v 6 The Lord guards the little ones —
\q2 I was humbled and he saved me.
\q1
\v 7 Turn back O my soul to your rest —
\q2 for the Lord dealt bountifully with you.
\q1
\v 8 For he rescued my soul from death —
\q2 my eyes from tears —
\q1 my feet from slipping.
\q1
\v 9 I will be well-pleasing before the Lord —
\q2 in the land of the living.

\c 115
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 I believed — therefore I spoke —
\q2 but I was greatly humbled.
\q1
\v 2 I said in my ecstasy:
\q2 Every man is a liar.
\q1
\v 3 What will I give back to the Lord —
\q2 for all that he gave back to me?
\q1
\v 4 A cup of salvation I will take —
\q2 and on the name of the Lord I will call.
\q1
\v 5 My vows to the Lord I will pay —
\q2 in the presence of all his people.
\q1
\v 6 Precious before the Lord —
\q2 is the death of his holy ones.
\q1
\v 7 O Lord I am your servant —
\q2 I am your servant and the son of your handmaiden —
\q1 you broke apart my bonds.
\q1
\v 8 To you I will sacrifice a sacrifice of praise —
\q2 and on the name of the Lord I will call.
\q1
\v 9 My vows to the Lord I will pay —
\q2 in the presence of all his people —
\q1
\v 10 in the courts of the house of the Lord —
\q2 in your midst O Jerusalem.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 116
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Praise the Lord all you nations —
\q2 praise him all you peoples.
\q1
\v 2 For his mercy was made mighty over us —
\q2 and the truth of the Lord endures to the age.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 117
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Give thanks to the Lord for he is good —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 2 Let the house of Israel now say:
\q2 For his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 3 Let the house of Aaron now say:
\q2 For his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 4 Let all those who fear the Lord now say:
\q2 For his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 5 Out of my distress I called upon the Lord —
\q2 and the Lord heard me and led me into broad space.
\q1
\v 6 The Lord is my helper — I will not fear —
\q2 what will man do to me?
\q1
\v 7 The Lord is my helper —
\q2 and I will look upon my enemies.
\q1
\v 8 It is better to hope in the Lord —
\q2 than to hope in man.
\q1
\v 9 It is better to hope in the Lord —
\q2 than to hope in rulers.
\q1
\v 10 All the nations surrounded me —
\q2 and in the name of the Lord I defended myself against them.
\q1
\v 11 Surrounding they surrounded me —
\q2 and in the name of the Lord I defended myself against them.
\q1
\v 12 They surrounded me as bees surrounding a honeycomb —
\q2 and they burst into flame as fire among thorns —
\q1 and in the name of the Lord I defended myself against them.
\q1
\v 13 I was pushed to fall —
\q2 but the Lord helped me.
\q1
\v 14 My strength and my song is the Lord —
\q2 and he became my salvation.
\q1
\v 15 A voice of rejoicing and salvation —
\q2 in the tents of the righteous.
\q1 The right hand of the Lord did power —
\q2
\v 16 the right hand of the Lord exalted me —
\q1 the right hand of the Lord did power.
\q1
\v 17 I will not die — I will live —
\q2 and recount the works of the Lord.
\q1
\v 18 The Lord disciplined me with discipline —
\q2 and did not give me over to death.
\q1
\v 19 Open to me the gates of righteousness —
\q2 entering through them I will confess to the Lord.
\q1
\v 20 This is the gate of the Lord —
\q2 the righteous will enter through it.
\q1
\v 21 I will confess to you — for you heard me —
\q2 and became my salvation.
\q1
\v 22 The stone which the builders rejected —
\q2 this one became the head of the corner.
\q1
\v 23 This came from the Lord —
\q2 and it is wonderful in our eyes.
\q1
\v 24 This is the day which the Lord made —
\q2 let us rejoice and be glad in it.
\q1
\v 25 O Lord save now —
\q2 O Lord prosper now.
\q1
\v 26 Blessed is the one who comes in the name of the Lord —
\q2 we blessed you from the house of the Lord.
\q1
\v 27 God is the Lord and he shone upon us —
\q2 appoint a festival with dense branches —
\q1 up to the horns of the altar.
\q1
\v 28 You are my God and I will confess to you —
\q2 my God and I will exalt you.
\q1
\v 29 Give thanks to the Lord for he is good —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.

\c 118
\d Hallelujah.
\q1 \v 1
\b Aleph
\q1 Blessed are the blameless in the way —
\q2 who walk in the law of the Lord.
\q1
\v 2 Blessed are those who search out his testimonies —
\q2 with a whole heart they will seek him.
\q1
\v 3 For those who work lawlessness —
\q2 did not walk in his ways.
\q1
\v 4 You commanded your commandments —
\q2 to be kept greatly.
\q1
\v 5 Would that my ways were directed —
\q2 to keep your ordinances.
\q1
\v 6 Then I will not be put to shame —
\q2 when I look upon all your commandments.
\q1
\v 7 I will confess to you in uprightness of heart —
\q2 when I learn the judgments of your righteousness.
\q1
\v 8 I will keep your ordinances —
\q2 do not forsake me greatly.
\q1 \v 9
\b Beth
\q1 How will a young man straighten his way? —
\q2 by keeping your words.
\q1
\v 10 With my whole heart I sought you —
\q2 do not thrust me away from your commandments.
\q1
\v 11 In my heart I hid your words —
\q2 so that I may not sin against you.
\q1
\v 12 Blessed are you O Lord —
\q2 teach me your ordinances.
\q1
\v 13 With my lips I declared —
\q2 all the judgments of your mouth.
\q1
\v 14 In the way of your testimonies I rejoiced —
\q2 as over all riches.
\q1
\v 15 In your commandments I will meditate —
\q2 and I will understand your ways.
\q1
\v 16 In your ordinances I will meditate —
\q2 I will not forget your words.
\q1 \v 17
\b Gimel
\q1 Repay your servant — give me life —
\q2 and I will keep your words.
\q1
\v 18 Uncover my eyes —
\q2 and I will understand the wonders from your law.
\q1
\v 19 I am a sojourner upon the earth —
\q2 do not hide your commandments from me.
\q1
\v 20 My soul longed to desire —
\q2 your judgments at all times.
\q1
\v 21 You rebuked the proud —
\q2 cursed are those who turn aside from your commandments.
\q1
\v 22 Take away from me reproach and contempt —
\q2 for I sought out your testimonies.
\q1
\v 23 For rulers sat and spoke against me —
\q2 but your servant was exercised in your ordinances.
\q1
\v 24 For your testimonies are my meditation —
\q2 and your ordinances are my counselors.
\q1 \v 25
\b Daleth
\q1 My soul cleaved to the ground —
\q2 give me life according to your word.
\q1
\v 26 I declared my ways and you heard me —
\q2 teach me your ordinances.
\q1
\v 27 Make me understand the way of your ordinances —
\q2 and I will meditate on your wonders.
\q1
\v 28 My soul dropped from grief —
\q2 strengthen me with your words.
\q1
\v 29 Remove from me the way of lawlessness —
\q2 and with your law have mercy on me.
\q1
\v 30 I chose the way of truth —
\q2 I did not forget your judgments.
\q1
\v 31 I cleaved to your testimonies O Lord —
\q2 do not put me to shame.
\q1
\v 32 I ran in the way of your commandments —
\q2 when you broadened my heart.
\q1 \v 33
\b He
\q1 Set before me O Lord the way of your ordinances —
\q2 and I will seek it always.
\q1
\v 34 Give me understanding and I will search out your law —
\q2 and I will keep it with my whole heart.
\q1
\v 35 Lead me in the path of your commandments —
\q2 for I desired it.
\q1
\v 36 Incline my heart to your testimonies —
\q2 and not to covetousness.
\q1
\v 37 Turn away my eyes so as not to see vanity —
\q2 give me life in your way.
\q1
\v 38 Establish your word to your servant —
\q2 for your fear.
\q1
\v 39 Take away my reproach which I feared —
\q2 for your judgments are good.
\q1
\v 40 Look — I desired your commandments —
\q2 in your righteousness give me life.
\q1 \v 41
\b Waw
\q1 And let your mercy come upon me O Lord —
\q2 your salvation according to your word.
\q1
\v 42 And I will answer those who reproach me a word —
\q2 for I hoped in your words.
\q1
\v 43 And do not take the word of truth entirely from my mouth —
\q2 for I hoped in your judgments.
\q1
\v 44 And I will keep your law always —
\q2 to the age and beyond.
\q1
\v 45 And I walked in broad space —
\q2 because I sought out your commandments.
\q1
\v 46 And I spoke of your testimonies before kings —
\q2 and I was not put to shame.
\q1
\v 47 And I meditated on your commandments which I loved —
\q2
\v 48 and I lifted up my hands to your commandments which I loved —
\q1 and I meditated on your ordinances.
\q1 \v 49
\b Zayin
\q1 Remember your word to your servant —
\q2 in which you made me hope.
\q1
\v 50 This comforted me in my humiliation —
\q2 for your word gave me life.
\q1
\v 51 The proud transgressed greatly —
\q2 but from your law I did not turn aside.
\q1
\v 52 I remembered your judgments from the age O Lord —
\q2 and I was comforted.
\q1
\v 53 Despondency seized me because of the sinners —
\q2 those who forsake your law.
\q1
\v 54 Your ordinances were songs to me —
\q2 in the place of my sojourning.
\q1
\v 55 I remembered your name at night O Lord —
\q2 and kept your law.
\q1
\v 56 This became mine —
\q2 because I sought out your ordinances.
\q1 \v 57
\b Heth
\q1 My portion is the Lord — I said —
\q2 to keep your words.
\q1
\v 58 I entreated your face with my whole heart —
\q2 have mercy on me according to your word.
\q1
\v 59 I considered your ways —
\q2 and turned my feet to your testimonies.
\q1
\v 60 I was ready and was not troubled —
\q2 to keep your commandments.
\q1
\v 61 Cords of sinners entangled me —
\q2 but I did not forget your law.
\q1
\v 62 At midnight I rose to give thanks to you —
\q2 for the judgments of your righteousness.
\q1
\v 63 I am a companion of all who fear you —
\q2 and of those who keep your commandments.
\q1
\v 64 O Lord the earth is full of your mercy —
\q2 teach me your ordinances.
\q1 \v 65
\b Teth
\q1 You did good to your servant O Lord —
\q2 according to your word.
\q1
\v 66 Teach me goodness and instruction and knowledge —
\q2 for I believed in your commandments.
\q1
\v 67 Before I was humbled I transgressed —
\q2 therefore I kept your word.
\q1
\v 68 Good are you O Lord —
\q2 and in your goodness teach me your ordinances.
\q1
\v 69 The lawlessness of the proud multiplied over me —
\q2 but I will search out your commandments with my whole heart.
\q1
\v 70 Their heart curdled as milk —
\q2 but I meditated on your law.
\q1
\v 71 It is good for me that you humbled me —
\q2 so that I may learn your ordinances.
\q1
\v 72 The law of your mouth is better to me —
\q2 than thousands of gold and silver.
\q1 \v 73
\b Yod
\q1 Your hands made me and formed me —
\q2 give me understanding and I will learn your commandments.
\q1
\v 74 Those who fear you will see me and be glad —
\q2 because I hoped in your words.
\q1
\v 75 I know O Lord that your judgments are righteousness —
\q2 and in truth you humbled me.
\q1
\v 76 Let your mercy be to comfort me —
\q2 according to your word to your servant.
\q1
\v 77 Let your compassions come to me and I will live —
\q2 for your law is my meditation.
\q1
\v 78 Let the proud be put to shame — for lawlessly they perverted me —
\q2 but I will exercise myself in your commandments.
\q1
\v 79 Let those who fear you turn to me —
\q2 and those who know your testimonies.
\q1
\v 80 Let my heart be blameless in your ordinances —
\q2 so that I may not be put to shame.
\q1 \v 81
\b Kaph
\q1 My soul fainted for your salvation —
\q2 in your word I hoped.
\q1
\v 82 My eyes failed for your word —
\q2 saying: When will you comfort me?
\q1
\v 83 For I became as a wineskin in the frost —
\q2 I did not forget your ordinances.
\q1
\v 84 How many are the days of your servant —
\q2 when will you execute judgment on those who pursue me?
\q1
\v 85 Lawless ones recounted fables to me —
\q2 but not as your law O Lord.
\q1
\v 86 All your commandments are truth —
\q2 they persecuted me unjustly — help me.
\q1
\v 87 They almost destroyed me upon the earth —
\q2 but I did not forsake your commandments.
\q1
\v 88 According to your mercy give me life —
\q2 and I will keep the testimonies of your mouth.
\q1 \v 89
\b Lamedh
\q1 To the age O Lord —
\q2 your word stands firm in heaven.
\q1
\v 90 To generation and generation is your truth —
\q2 you founded the earth and it stands.
\q1
\v 91 By your ordinance the day continues —
\q2 for all things serve you.
\q1
\v 92 If your law had not been my meditation —
\q2 I would have perished then in my humiliation.
\q1
\v 93 To the age I will not forget your ordinances —
\q2 for in them you gave me life.
\q1
\v 94 I am yours — save me —
\q2 for I sought out your ordinances.
\q1
\v 95 Sinners waited for me to destroy me —
\q2 but I understood your testimonies.
\q1
\v 96 I saw an end to all completion —
\q2 your commandment is very broad.
\q1 \v 97
\b Mem
\q1 How I loved your law —
\q2 all the day it is my meditation.
\q1
\v 98 Beyond my enemies you made me wiser with your commandment —
\q2 for it is mine to the age.
\q1
\v 99 Beyond all who teach me I understood —
\q2 for your testimonies are my meditation.
\q1
\v 100 Beyond elders I understood —
\q2 because I sought out your commandments.
\q1
\v 101 From every evil way I kept back my feet —
\q2 so that I may keep your words.
\q1
\v 102 From your judgments I did not turn aside —
\q2 for you set a law for me.
\q1
\v 103 How sweet are your words to my throat —
\q2 beyond honey to my mouth.
\q1
\v 104 From your commandments I understood —
\q2 therefore I hated every way of lawlessness.
\q1 \v 105
\b Nun
\q1 A lamp to my feet is your word —
\q2 and a light to my paths.
\q1
\v 106 I swore and confirmed —
\q2 to keep the judgments of your righteousness.
\q1
\v 107 I was humbled greatly O Lord —
\q2 give me life according to your word.
\q1
\v 108 The willing offerings of my mouth accept O Lord —
\q2 and teach me your judgments.
\q1
\v 109 My soul is in your hands always —
\q2 and I did not forget your law.
\q1
\v 110 Sinners set a snare for me —
\q2 but from your commandments I did not go astray.
\q1
\v 111 I inherited your testimonies to the age —
\q2 for they are the rejoicing of my heart.
\q1
\v 112 I inclined my heart to do your ordinances —
\q2 to the age — for the reward.
\q1 \v 113
\b Samekh
\q1 I hated the lawless ones —
\q2 but I loved your law.
\q1
\v 114 You are my helper and my supporter —
\q2 I hoped in your words.
\q1
\v 115 Turn away from me you evil ones —
\q2 and I will search out the commandments of my God.
\q1
\v 116 Support me according to your word and give me life —
\q2 and do not put me to shame from my hope.
\q1
\v 117 Help me and I will be saved —
\q2 and I will meditate on your ordinances always.
\q1
\v 118 You made light of all who turned from your ordinances —
\q2 for their devising is lawlessness.
\q1
\v 119 I reckoned as transgressors all the sinners of the earth —
\q2 therefore I loved your testimonies.
\q1
\v 120 Nail down my flesh with the fear of you —
\q2 for I was afraid of your judgments.
\q1 \v 121
\b Ayin
\q1 I did judgment and righteousness —
\q2 do not give me over to those who wrong me.
\q1
\v 122 Accept your servant for good —
\q2 let not the proud wrong me.
\q1
\v 123 My eyes failed for your salvation —
\q2 and for the word of your righteousness.
\q1
\v 124 Deal with your servant according to your mercy —
\q2 and teach me your ordinances.
\q1
\v 125 I am your servant — give me understanding —
\q2 and I will know your testimonies.
\q1
\v 126 It is time for the Lord to act —
\q2 they scattered your law.
\q1
\v 127 Therefore I loved your commandments —
\q2 beyond gold and topaz.
\q1
\v 128 Therefore I directed myself according to all your commandments —
\q2 I hated every way of lawlessness.
\q1 \v 129
\b Pe
\q1 Your testimonies are wonderful —
\q2 therefore my soul searched them out.
\q1
\v 130 The unfolding of your words gives light —
\q2 and gives understanding to the little ones.
\q1
\v 131 I opened my mouth and drew breath —
\q2 for I longed for your commandments.
\q1
\v 132 Look upon me and have mercy on me —
\q2 as is the judgment of those who love your name.
\q1
\v 133 Direct my steps according to your word —
\q2 and let no lawlessness rule over me.
\q1
\v 134 Ransom-release me from the slander of men —
\q2 and I will keep your commandments.
\q1
\v 135 Make your face shine upon your servant —
\q2 and teach me your ordinances.
\q1
\v 136 Streams of water came down from my eyes —
\q2 because they did not keep your law.
\q1 \v 137
\b Tsade
\q1 Righteous are you O Lord —
\q2 and upright are your judgments.
\q1
\v 138 You commanded righteousness — your testimonies —
\q2 and great faithfulness.
\q1
\v 139 My zeal consumed me —
\q2 because my enemies forgot your words.
\q1
\v 140 Your word was tried with fire to the uttermost —
\q2 and your servant loved it.
\q1
\v 141 I am young and despised —
\q2 but your ordinances I did not forget.
\q1
\v 142 Your righteousness is righteousness to the age —
\q2 and your law is truth.
\q1
\v 143 Afflictions and necessities found me —
\q2 your commandments are my meditation.
\q1
\v 144 The righteousness of your testimonies is to the age —
\q2 give me understanding and I will live.
\q1 \v 145
\b Qoph
\q1 I cried with my whole heart — hear me O Lord —
\q2 I will seek out your ordinances.
\q1
\v 146 I cried to you — save me —
\q2 and I will keep your testimonies.
\q1
\v 147 I went before in the dawn and cried —
\q2 in your words I hoped.
\q1
\v 148 My eyes went before the morning watches —
\q2 to meditate on your words.
\q1
\v 149 Hear my voice O Lord according to your mercy —
\q2 give me life according to your judgment.
\q1
\v 150 Those who pursue lawlessness drew near —
\q2 they were far from your law.
\q1
\v 151 You are near O Lord —
\q2 and all your commandments are truth.
\q1
\v 152 From the beginning I knew from your testimonies —
\q2 that you founded them to the age.
\q1 \v 153
\b Resh
\q1 See my humiliation and rescue me —
\q2 for I did not forget your law.
\q1
\v 154 Plead my cause and ransom-release me —
\q2 give me life because of your word.
\q1
\v 155 Salvation is far from sinners —
\q2 for they did not seek out your ordinances.
\q1
\v 156 Your compassions are many O Lord —
\q2 give me life according to your judgment.
\q1
\v 157 Many are those who pursue me and oppress me —
\q2 from your testimonies I did not turn aside.
\q1
\v 158 I saw the men without understanding and I melted —
\q2 because they did not keep your words.
\q1
\v 159 See how I loved your commandments O Lord —
\q2 in your mercy give me life.
\q1
\v 160 The beginning of your words is truth —
\q2 and all the judgments of your righteousness are to the age.
\q1 \v 161
\b Sin/Shin
\q1 Rulers persecuted me without cause —
\q2 and from your words my heart was in awe.
\q1
\v 162 I will rejoice over your words —
\q2 as one finding great spoil.
\q1
\v 163 Lawlessness I hated and abhorred —
\q2 but your law I loved.
\q1
\v 164 Seven times a day I praised you —
\q2 because of the judgments of your righteousness.
\q1
\v 165 Great peace for those who love your law —
\q2 and there is no stumbling-block for them.
\q1
\v 166 I waited for your salvation O Lord —
\q2 and your commandments I loved.
\q1
\v 167 My soul kept your testimonies —
\q2 and I loved them greatly.
\q1
\v 168 I kept your commandments and your testimonies —
\q2 for all my ways are before you.
\q1 \v 169
\b Taw
\q1 Let my petition draw near before you O Lord —
\q2 give me understanding according to your word.
\q1
\v 170 Let my supplication enter before you —
\q2 rescue me according to your word.
\q1
\v 171 My lips will utter praise —
\q2 when you teach me your ordinances.
\q1
\v 172 My tongue will respond to your word —
\q2 for all your commandments are righteousness.
\q1
\v 173 Let your hand be to save me —
\q2 for I chose your commandments.
\q1
\v 174 I longed for your salvation O Lord —
\q2 and your law is my meditation.
\q1
\v 175 My soul will live and will praise you —
\q2 and your judgments will help me.
\q1
\v 176 I went astray as a lost sheep — seek your servant —
\q2 for I did not forget your commandments.

\c 119
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 To the Lord when I was afflicted I cried —
\q2 and he heard me.
\q1
\v 2 O Lord rescue my soul from unjust lips —
\q2 and from a deceitful tongue.
\q1
\v 3 What will be given to you or what will be added to you —
\q2 against a deceitful tongue?
\q1
\v 4 The sharp arrows of the mighty one —
\q2 with the coals of the desolate-place.
\q1
\v 5 Woe to me — for my sojourning was prolonged —
\q2 I dwelt among the tents of Kedar.
\q1
\v 6 Much did my soul sojourn —
\q2
\v 7 I was peaceable with those who hated peace —
\q1 when I spoke to them they fought against me without cause.

\c 120
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 I lifted up my eyes to the mountains —
\q2 from where will my help come?
\q1
\v 2 My help is from the Lord —
\q2 who made heaven and earth.
\q1
\v 3 May he not give your foot to shaking —
\q2 nor your guardian slumber.
\q1
\v 4 Look — he will not slumber nor sleep —
\q2 the guardian of Israel.
\q1
\v 5 The Lord will guard you —
\q2 the Lord is your shelter upon your right hand.
\q1
\v 6 By day the sun will not burn you —
\q2 nor the moon by night.
\q1
\v 7 The Lord will guard you from all evil —
\q2 the Lord will guard your soul.
\q1
\v 8 The Lord will guard your coming in and your going out —
\q2 from now and to the age.

\c 121
\d A song of ascents — of David.
\q1
\v 1 I was glad when they said to me:
\q2 We will go into the house of the Lord.
\q1
\v 2 Our feet were standing —
\q2 in your courts O Jerusalem.
\q1
\v 3 Jerusalem — built as a city —
\q2 whose partaking is together.
\q1
\v 4 For there the tribes went up —
\q2 the tribes of the Lord —
\q1 a testimony for Israel —
\q2 to give thanks to the name of the Lord.
\q1
\v 5 For there thrones for judgment were set —
\q2 thrones for the house of David.
\q1
\v 6 Ask now for the peace of Jerusalem —
\q2 and abundance for those who love you.
\q1
\v 7 Let peace be in your power —
\q2 and abundance in your towers.
\q1
\v 8 For the sake of my brothers and my neighbors —
\q2 I spoke peace concerning you.
\q1
\v 9 For the sake of the house of the Lord our God —
\q2 I sought good for you.

\c 122
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 To you I lifted up my eyes —
\q2 O you who dwell in heaven.
\q1
\v 2 Look — as the eyes of servants —
\q2 are upon the hands of their masters —
\q1 as the eyes of a handmaiden —
\q2 are upon the hands of her mistress —
\q1 so our eyes are upon the Lord our God —
\q2 until he has mercy on us.
\q1
\v 3 Have mercy on us O Lord — have mercy on us —
\q2 for we were greatly filled with contempt.
\q1
\v 4 Our soul was greatly filled —
\q2 the reproach of those who prosper —
\q1 and the contempt for the proud.

\c 123
\d A song of ascents — of David.
\q1
\v 1 If the Lord had not been among us —
\q2 let Israel now say —
\q1
\v 2 if the Lord had not been among us —
\q2 when men rose against us —
\q1 they would have swallowed us alive —
\q2
\v 3 when their anger burned against us —
\q1 perhaps the water would have drowned us.
\q1
\v 4 Our soul passed through the torrent —
\q2 perhaps our soul would have passed through the water that cannot be endured.
\q1
\v 5 Blessed is the Lord —
\q2 who did not give us as prey to their teeth.
\q1
\v 6 Our soul was rescued as a sparrow from the snare of the hunters —
\q2 the snare was broken and we were rescued.
\q1
\v 7 Our help is in the name of the Lord —
\q2 who made heaven and earth.

\c 124
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 Those who trust in the Lord are as Mount Zion —
\q2 the one who dwells in Jerusalem will not be shaken to the age.
\q1
\v 2 Mountains are around her —
\q2 and the Lord is around his people —
\q1 from now and to the age.
\q1
\v 3 For the Lord will not leave the rod of sinners —
\q2 upon the lot of the righteous —
\q1 so that the righteous may not stretch out their hands to lawlessness.
\q1
\v 4 Do good O Lord to the good —
\q2 and to the upright in heart.
\q1
\v 5 But those who turn to crooked ways —
\q2 the Lord will lead them away with those who work lawlessness —
\q1 peace upon Israel.

\c 125
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 When the Lord turned back the captivity of Zion —
\q2 we became as comforted ones.
\q1
\v 2 Then our mouth was filled with gladness —
\q2 and our tongue with rejoicing.
\q1 Then they said among the nations:
\q2 The Lord did great things among them.
\q1
\v 3 The Lord did great things among us —
\q2 we became glad.
\q1
\v 4 Turn back O Lord our captivity —
\q2 as the torrents in the south.
\q1
\v 5 Those who sow with tears —
\q2 will reap with rejoicing.
\q1
\v 6 Going they went and wept —
\q2 casting their seeds —
\q1 but coming they will come with rejoicing —
\q2 carrying their sheaves.

\c 126
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 If the Lord does not build the house —
\q2 in vain the builders labored.
\q1 If the Lord does not guard the city —
\q2 in vain the guard stayed awake.
\q1
\v 2 It is vain for you to rise before light —
\q2 rise after sitting — you who eat the bread of grief —
\q1 when he gives sleep to his beloved ones.
\q1
\v 3 Look — the inheritance from the Lord is sons —
\q2 the reward of the fruit of the womb.
\q1
\v 4 As arrows in the hand of the mighty one —
\q2 so are the sons of those who were shaken out.
\q1
\v 5 Blessed is the man who fills his desire with them —
\q2 he will not be put to shame when he speaks with his enemies at the gate.

\c 127
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 Blessed are all who fear the Lord —
\q2 who walk in his ways.
\q1
\v 2 The labors of your hands you will eat —
\q2 you are blessed and it will be well with you.
\q1
\v 3 Your wife is as a fruitful vine —
\q2 on the sides of your house.
\q1 Your sons as new shoots of the olive —
\q2 around your table.
\q1
\v 4 Look — so will the man be blessed —
\q2 who fears the Lord.
\q1
\v 5 The Lord will bless you from Zion —
\q2 and you will see the good of Jerusalem —
\q1 all the days of your life.
\q1
\v 6 And you will see the sons of your sons —
\q2 peace upon Israel.

\c 128
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 Many times they fought against me from my youth —
\q2 let Israel now say —
\q1
\v 2 many times they fought against me from my youth —
\q2 and yet they did not prevail against me.
\q1
\v 3 Upon my back the sinners forged —
\q2 they prolonged their lawlessness.
\q1
\v 4 The Lord is righteous —
\q2 he cut the necks of sinners.
\q1
\v 5 Let all who hate Zion be put to shame and turned back.
\q2
\v 6 Let them be as grass upon the housetops —
\q1 which withered before it was pulled up —
\q2
\v 7 with which the one who reaps did not fill his hand —
\q1 nor the one who gathers sheaves his bosom —
\q2
\v 8 and those who passed by did not say:
\q1 The blessing of the Lord be upon you —
\q2 we blessed you in the name of the Lord.

\c 129
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 From the depths I cried to you O Lord —
\q2
\v 2 O Lord hear my voice.
\q1 Let your ears be attentive —
\q2 to the voice of my supplication.
\q1
\v 3 If you keep watch over lawless deeds O Lord —
\q2 O Lord who will stand?
\q1
\v 4 For with you is forgiveness —
\q2 because of your law I waited for you O Lord.
\q1
\v 5 My soul waited upon his word —
\q2 my soul hoped in the Lord.
\q1
\v 6 From the morning watch until night —
\q2 from the morning watch let Israel hope in the Lord.
\q1
\v 7 For with the Lord is mercy —
\q2 and with him is abundant ransom-release.
\q1
\v 8 And he himself will ransom-release Israel —
\q2 from all his lawless deeds.

\c 130
\d A song of ascents — of David.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord remember David —
\q2 and all his gentleness —
\q1
\v 2 as he swore to the Lord —
\q2 and vowed to the God of Jacob:
\q1
\v 3 I will not enter into the tent of my house —
\q2 nor go up onto the couch of my bed —
\q1
\v 4 I will not give sleep to my eyes —
\q2 nor slumber to my eyelids —
\q1 nor rest to my temples —
\q2
\v 5 until I find a place for the Lord —
\q1 a tabernacle for the God of Jacob.
\q1
\v 6 Look — we heard it in Ephrathah —
\q2 we found it in the fields of the forest.
\q1
\v 7 We will enter into his tabernacles —
\q2 we will bow down to the place where his feet stood.
\q1
\v 8 Arise O Lord into your rest —
\q2 you and the ark of your holiness.
\q1
\v 9 Your priests will be clothed with righteousness —
\q2 and your holy ones will rejoice.
\q1
\v 10 For the sake of David your servant —
\q2 do not turn away the face of your anointed.
\q1
\v 11 The Lord swore truth to David —
\q2 and will not turn away from it:
\q1 From the fruit of your belly —
\q2 I will set upon your throne.
\q1
\v 12 If your sons keep my covenant —
\q2 and these my testimonies which I will teach them —
\q1 also their sons to the age —
\q2 will sit upon your throne.
\q1
\v 13 For the Lord chose Zion —
\q2 he desired it for his dwelling:
\q1
\v 14 This is my rest to the age of ages —
\q2 here I will dwell — for I desired it.
\q1
\v 15 Her provision I will greatly bless —
\q2 her poor I will satisfy with bread.
\q1
\v 16 Her priests I will clothe with salvation —
\q2 and her holy ones will rejoice greatly.
\q1
\v 17 There I will make a horn spring up for David —
\q2 I prepared a lamp for my anointed.
\q1
\v 18 His enemies I will clothe with shame —
\q2 but upon him my holiness will flourish.

\c 131
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord my heart was not exalted —
\q2 nor were my eyes lifted up —
\q1 nor did I walk in great things —
\q2 nor in wonders beyond me.
\q1
\v 2 If I was not humble-minded —
\q2 but exalted my soul —
\q1 as a weaned child upon his mother —
\q2 so will you recompense my soul.
\q1
\v 3 Let Israel hope in the Lord —
\q2 from now and to the age.

\c 132
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 Look — how good and how pleasant —
\q2 for brothers to dwell together.
\q1
\v 2 As oil upon the head —
\q2 that comes down upon the beard —
\q1 the beard of Aaron —
\q2 that comes down upon the collar of his garment —
\q1
\v 3 as the dew of Hermon —
\q2 that comes down upon the mountains of Zion.
\q1 For there the Lord commanded the blessing —
\q2 life to the age.

\c 133
\d A song of ascents.
\q1
\v 1 Look — bless the Lord —
\q2 all you servants of the Lord —
\q1 who stand in the house of the Lord —
\q2 in the courts of the house of our God.
\q1
\v 2 Lift up your hands to the holy places —
\q2 and bless the Lord.
\q1
\v 3 May the Lord bless you from Zion —
\q2 the one who made heaven and earth.

\c 134
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Praise the name of the Lord —
\q2 praise O servants of the Lord —
\q1
\v 2 you who stand in the house of the Lord —
\q2 in the courts of the house of our God.
\q1
\v 3 Praise the Lord — for the Lord is good —
\q2 sing a psalm to his name — for it is pleasant.
\q1
\v 4 For the Lord chose Jacob for himself —
\q2 Israel for his prized possession.
\q1
\v 5 For I know that the Lord is great —
\q2 and our Lord is above all gods.
\q1
\v 6 All things whatever the Lord willed he did —
\q2 in heaven and on earth —
\q1 in the seas and in all the depths.
\q1
\v 7 He brings up clouds from the ends of the earth —
\q2 lightnings for rain he made —
\q1 who brings out wind from his treasuries.
\q1
\v 8 Who struck the firstborn of Egypt —
\q2 from man to animal.
\q1
\v 9 He sent signs and wonders into your midst O Egypt —
\q2 against Pharaoh and against all his servants.
\q1
\v 10 Who struck many nations —
\q2 and killed mighty kings —
\q1
\v 11 Sihon king of the Amorites —
\q2 and Og king of Bashan —
\q1 and all the kingdoms of Canaan —
\q2
\v 12 and gave their land as an inheritance —
\q1 an inheritance to Israel his people.
\q1
\v 13 O Lord your name is to the age —
\q2 and your memorial to generation and generation.
\q1
\v 14 For the Lord will judge his people —
\q2 and upon his servants he will have compassion.
\q1
\v 15 The idols of the nations are silver and gold —
\q2 works of men's hands.
\q1
\v 16 They have a mouth and will not speak —
\q2 they have eyes and will not see —
\q1
\v 17 they have ears and will not hear —
\q2 for there is no breath in their mouth.
\q1
\v 18 Let those who make them become like them —
\q2 and all who trust in them.
\q1
\v 19 House of Israel — bless the Lord —
\q2 house of Aaron — bless the Lord —
\q1
\v 20 house of Levi — bless the Lord —
\q2 you who fear the Lord — bless the Lord.
\q1
\v 21 Blessed is the Lord from Zion —
\q2 the one who dwells in Jerusalem.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 135
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Give thanks to the Lord for he is good —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 2 Give thanks to the God of gods —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 3 Give thanks to the Lord of lords —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 4 To him who alone does great wonders —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 5 To him who made the heavens with understanding —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 6 To him who founded the earth upon the waters —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 7 To him who made the great lights —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 8 The sun for authority over the day —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 9 The moon and the stars for authority over the night —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 10 To him who struck Egypt in their firstborn —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 11 And led out Israel from among them —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 12 With a strong hand and a high arm —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 13 To him who divided the Red Sea into portions —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 14 And led Israel through the midst of it —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 15 And shook off Pharaoh and his force into the Red Sea —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 16 To him who led his people through the great desolate-place —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 17 To him who struck great kings —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 18 And killed mighty kings —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 19 Sihon king of the Amorites —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 20 And Og king of Bashan —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 21 And gave their land as an inheritance —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 22 An inheritance to Israel his servant —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 23 For in our humiliation he remembered us —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 24 And ransomed-released us from our enemies —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 25 Who gives food to all flesh —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.
\q1
\v 26 Give thanks to the God of heaven —
\q2 for his mercy is to the age.

\c 136
\d Of David.
\q1
\v 1 By the rivers of Babylon —
\q2 there we sat and wept —
\q1 when we remembered Zion.
\q1
\v 2 Upon the willows in her midst —
\q2 we hung our harps.
\q1
\v 3 For there those who had taken us captive asked us —
\q2 words of song —
\q1 and those who had led us away —
\q2 a hymn:
\q1 Sing us one of the songs of Zion.
\q1
\v 4 How will we sing the song of the Lord —
\q2 in a foreign land?
\q1
\v 5 If I forget you O Jerusalem —
\q2 let my right hand be forgotten.
\q1
\v 6 Let my tongue cleave to my throat —
\q2 if I do not remember you —
\q1 if I do not set Jerusalem —
\q2 as the beginning of my gladness.
\q1
\v 7 Remember O Lord the sons of Edom —
\q2 in the day of Jerusalem —
\q1 who said: Lay it bare — lay it bare —
\q2 to its foundations.
\q1
\v 8 O daughter of Babylon — wretched one —
\q2 blessed is the one who will repay you —
\q1 your recompense with which you repaid us.
\q1
\v 9 Blessed is the one who will seize —
\q2 and dash your little ones against the rock.

\c 137
\d Of David.
\q1
\v 1 I will confess to you O Lord with my whole heart —
\q2 for you heard the words of my mouth —
\q1 and before the angels I will make a psalm to you.
\q1
\v 2 I will bow down toward your holy temple —
\q2 and I will confess to your name —
\q1 for your mercy and your truth —
\q2 for you magnified your holy name above all.
\q1
\v 3 In whatever day I call upon you — hear me —
\q2 you will multiply strength in my soul.
\q1
\v 4 Let all the kings of the earth confess to you O Lord —
\q2 for they heard all the words of your mouth.
\q1
\v 5 And let them sing in the ways of the Lord —
\q2 for great is the glory of the Lord.
\q1
\v 6 For the Lord is high and looks upon the lowly —
\q2 and the exalted one he knows from afar.
\q1
\v 7 If I walk in the midst of affliction you will give me life —
\q2 against the wrath of my enemies you stretched out your hand —
\q1 and your right hand saved me.
\q1
\v 8 The Lord will complete things on my behalf —
\q2 O Lord your mercy is to the age —
\q1 the works of your hands do not disregard.

\c 138
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord you searched me and knew me —
\q2
\v 2 you yourself knew my sitting and my rising.
\q1 You understood my thoughts from afar —
\q2
\v 3 you traced my path and my resting-place —
\q1 and all my ways you foresaw.
\q1
\v 4 For there is no word on my tongue —
\q2 look — O Lord you know all things.
\q1
\v 5 You formed me behind and before —
\q2 and you laid your hand upon me.
\q1
\v 6 The knowledge is too wonderful for me —
\q2 it was strengthened — I will not be able to reach it.
\q1
\v 7 Where will I go from your breath-of-life —
\q2 and where will I flee from your face?
\q1
\v 8 If I go up to heaven — you are there —
\q2 if I go down to Hades — you are present.
\q1
\v 9 If I take up my wings toward the east —
\q2 and dwell at the furthest parts of the sea —
\q1
\v 10 even there your hand will lead me —
\q2 and your right hand will hold me.
\q1
\v 11 And I said: Surely darkness will trample me down —
\q2 and night will be light in my delight —
\q1
\v 12 for darkness will not be dark to you —
\q2 and night will be illuminated as day —
\q1 as its darkness — so its light.
\q1
\v 13 For you possessed my kidneys —
\q2 you supported me from my mother's womb.
\q1
\v 14 I will confess to you — for fearfully I was made to be wonderful —
\q2 wonderful are your works —
\q1 and my soul knows this well.
\q1
\v 15 My frame was not hidden from you —
\q2 which was made in secret —
\q1 and my substance in the lowest parts of the earth.
\q1
\v 16 Your eyes saw my unformed state —
\q2 and in your book they will all be written —
\q1 the days that were formed —
\q2 and not one among them.
\q1
\v 17 But to me how greatly your friends were honored O God —
\q2 how greatly were their beginnings strengthened.
\q1
\v 18 I will count them — and beyond sand they will be multiplied —
\q2 I was roused and I am still with you.
\q1
\v 19 If you will kill the sinner O God —
\q2 O men of blood — depart from me.
\q1
\v 20 For you say toward your thought —
\q2 they will take your cities in vain.
\q1
\v 21 Did I not hate those who hate you O Lord —
\q2 and waste away because of your enemies?
\q1
\v 22 With perfect hatred I hated them —
\q2 they became enemies to me.
\q1
\v 23 Search me O God and know my heart —
\q2 test me and know my paths.
\q1
\v 24 And see if there is a way of lawlessness in me —
\q2 and lead me in the everlasting way.

\c 139
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 Rescue me O Lord from the evil man —
\q2 from the lawless man deliver me —
\q1
\v 2 who devised lawlessness in their heart —
\q2 all the day they prepared battles.
\q1
\v 3 They sharpened their tongue as a serpent's —
\q2 the poison of asps is under their lips.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 4 Guard me O Lord from the hand of the sinner —
\q2 from lawless men deliver me —
\q1 those who devised to trip up my steps.
\q1
\v 5 The proud hid a snare for me —
\q2 and cords — they spread a net by the way —
\q1 snares they set for me.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 6 I said to the Lord: You are my God —
\q2 hear O Lord the voice of my supplication.
\q1
\v 7 O Lord Lord — the strength of my salvation —
\q2 you overshadowed my head in the day of battle.
\q1
\v 8 Do not give me O Lord to the desire of the sinner —
\q2 they devised against me — do not abandon me —
\q1 lest they be exalted.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 9 The head of those surrounding me —
\q2 the labor of their lips will cover them.
\q1
\v 10 Coals of fire will fall upon them —
\q2 you will cast them down into fire —
\q1 in miseries — they will not stand.
\q1
\v 11 A man of tongue will not be established on the earth —
\q2 the evil one will hunt the lawless man for destruction.
\q1
\v 12 I know that the Lord will accomplish —
\q2 the judgment of the poor and the vengeance of the needy.
\q1
\v 13 Yet the righteous will confess to your name —
\q2 and the upright will dwell before your face.

\c 140
\d A Psalm of David.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord I cried to you — hear me —
\q2 give ear to my voice when I cry to you.
\q1
\v 2 Let my prayer be set right as incense before you —
\q2 the lifting up of my hands as an evening sacrifice.
\q1
\v 3 Set a guard O Lord over my mouth —
\q2 and a door of enclosure around my lips.
\q1
\v 4 Do not incline my heart to evil words —
\q2 to make excuses in sins —
\q1 with men who work lawlessness —
\q2 and I will not associate with their chosen ones.
\q1
\v 5 The righteous one will discipline me in mercy and rebuke me —
\q2 but the oil of the sinner let it not anoint my head —
\q1 for still my prayer is against their desires.
\q1
\v 6 Their judges were swallowed up beside a rock —
\q2 they will hear my words — for they were pleasant.
\q1
\v 7 As when the earth is split and shattered upon the ground —
\q2 so their bones were scattered at the mouth of Hades.
\q1
\v 8 For my eyes are toward you O Lord Lord —
\q2 in you I hoped — do not take away my soul.
\q1
\v 9 Guard me from the snare they set for me —
\q2 and from the traps of those who work lawlessness.
\q1
\v 10 The sinners will fall in his own net —
\q2 I am alone until I pass through.

\c 141
\d Understanding of David — a prayer when he was in the cave.
\q1
\v 1 With my voice I cried to the Lord —
\q2 with my voice to the Lord I made supplication.
\q1
\v 2 I poured out before him my petition —
\q2 my affliction before him I declared.
\q1
\v 3 When my breath-of-life fainted within me —
\q2 you knew my paths.
\q1 In the way in which I walked —
\q2 they hid a snare for me.
\q1
\v 4 I looked to the right and saw —
\q2 and there was none who knew me.
\q1 Flight perished from me —
\q2 and there was none who sought out my soul.
\q1
\v 5 I cried to you O Lord —
\q2 I said: You are my hope —
\q1 my portion in the land of the living.
\q1
\v 6 Attend to my petition —
\q2 for I was greatly humbled —
\q1 rescue me from those who pursue me —
\q2 for they are stronger than I.
\q1
\v 7 Lead out my soul from prison —
\q2 to confess to your name.
\q1 The righteous will wait for me —
\q2 until you repay me.

\c 142
\d A Psalm of David — when his son pursued him.
\q1
\v 1 O Lord hear my prayer —
\q2 give ear to my supplication in your truth —
\q1 hear me in your righteousness.
\q1
\v 2 And do not enter into judgment with your servant —
\q2 for every living one will not be justified before you.
\q1
\v 3 For the enemy pursued my soul —
\q2 he humbled my life to the ground —
\q1 he made me dwell in dark places —
\q2 as those who are dead to the age.
\q1
\v 4 And my breath-of-life was despondent within me —
\q2 within me my heart was troubled.
\q1
\v 5 I remembered the days of old —
\q2 I meditated on all your works —
\q1 in the works of your hands I exercised myself.
\q1
\v 6 I stretched out my hands to you —
\q2 my soul is as a waterless land before you.
\qs diapsalma\qs*
\q1
\v 7 Hear me quickly O Lord —
\q2 my breath-of-life failed —
\q1 do not turn away your face from me —
\q2 and I will be like those going down to the pit.
\q1
\v 8 Make me hear your mercy in the morning —
\q2 for in you I hoped —
\q1 make known to me the way in which I should walk —
\q2 for to you I lifted up my soul.
\q1
\v 9 Rescue me from my enemies O Lord —
\q2 for to you I fled for refuge.
\q1
\v 10 Teach me to do your will —
\q2 for you are my God —
\q1 your good breath-of-life will lead me —
\q2 in a straight land.
\q1
\v 11 For the sake of your name O Lord you will give me life —
\q2 in your righteousness you will lead my soul out of affliction.
\q1
\v 12 And in your mercy you will destroy my enemies —
\q2 and you will destroy all those who oppress my soul —
\q1 for I am your servant.

\c 143
\d A Psalm of David — against Goliath.
\q1
\v 1 Blessed is the Lord my God —
\q2 who trains my hands for battle —
\q1 and my fingers for war.
\q1
\v 2 My mercy and my refuge —
\q2 my supporter and my deliverer —
\q1 my protector and in him I hoped —
\q2 who subjects peoples under me.
\q1
\v 3 O Lord what is man that you were made known to him —
\q2 or the son of man that you reckon him?
\q1
\v 4 Man was made like vanity —
\q2 his days pass as a shadow.
\q1
\v 5 O Lord bow down your heavens and come down —
\q2 touch the mountains and they will smoke.
\q1
\v 6 Lighten lightning and you will scatter them —
\q2 send out your arrows and you will trouble them.
\q1
\v 7 Send your hand from on high —
\q2 rescue me and deliver me from many waters —
\q1 from the hand of sons of strangers —
\q2
\v 8 whose mouth spoke vanity —
\q1 and their right hand is a right hand of lawlessness.
\q1
\v 9 O God a new song I will sing to you —
\q2 on a ten-stringed psaltery I will make a psalm to you.
\q1
\v 10 Who gives salvation to kings —
\q2 who ransomed-released David his servant from the evil sword.
\q1
\v 11 Rescue me and deliver me from the hand of sons of strangers —
\q2 whose mouth spoke vanity —
\q1 and their right hand is a right hand of lawlessness —
\q2
\v 12 whose sons are as new plants growing in their youth —
\q1 their daughters adorned — decked out as the likeness of a temple —
\q2
\v 13 their storehouses full — pouring out from this into that —
\q1 their sheep fruitful — multiplying in their streets —
\q2
\v 14 their oxen fat —
\q1 no breaking of fence — nor going out —
\q2 nor crying in their streets.
\q1
\v 15 They called the people who have these things — blessed —
\q2 but blessed is the people whose God is the Lord.

\c 144
\d Praise — of David.
\q1
\v 1 I will exalt you my God my king —
\q2 and I will bless your name to the age and beyond.
\q1
\v 2 Every day I will bless you —
\q2 and I will praise your name to the age and beyond.
\q1
\v 3 Great is the Lord and greatly to be praised —
\q2 and his greatness has no end.
\q1
\v 4 Generation to generation will praise your works —
\q2 and will announce your power.
\q1
\v 5 The glorious splendor of your holiness they will speak —
\q2 and your wonders they will recount.
\q1
\v 6 And the power of your fearful things they will speak —
\q2 and your greatness they will recount.
\q1
\v 7 The memory of the abundance of your goodness they will pour forth —
\q2 and in your righteousness they will rejoice.
\q1
\v 8 Compassionate and merciful is the Lord —
\q2 patient and greatly merciful.
\q1
\v 9 Good is the Lord to all —
\q2 and his compassions are upon all his works.
\q1
\v 10 Let all your works confess to you O Lord —
\q2 and your holy ones bless you.
\q1
\v 11 The glory of your kingdom they will speak —
\q2 and your power they will recount —
\q1
\v 12 to make known to the sons of men your power —
\q2 and the glorious splendor of your kingdom.
\q1
\v 13 Your kingdom is a kingdom of all the ages —
\q2 and your rule is in every generation and generation.
\q1 The Lord is faithful in all his words —
\q2 and holy in all his works.
\q1
\v 14 The Lord supports all who fall —
\q2 and raises up all who are bowed down.
\q1
\v 15 The eyes of all hope in you —
\q2 and you give them their food in due season.
\q1
\v 16 You open your hand —
\q2 and fill every living thing with good pleasure.
\q1
\v 17 Righteous is the Lord in all his ways —
\q2 and holy in all his works.
\q1
\v 18 The Lord is near to all who call upon him —
\q2 to all who call upon him in truth.
\q1
\v 19 The will of those who fear him he will do —
\q2 and their petition he will hear and save them.
\q1
\v 20 The Lord guards all who love him —
\q2 and all the sinners he will destroy.
\q1
\v 21 The praise of the Lord my mouth will speak —
\q2 and let all flesh bless his holy name —
\q1 to the age and beyond.

\c 145
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Praise the Lord O my soul —
\q2
\v 2 I will praise the Lord in my life —
\q1 I will make a psalm to my God while I exist.
\q1
\v 3 Do not trust in rulers —
\q2 nor in the sons of men —
\q1 in whom there is no salvation.
\q1
\v 4 His breath-of-life will go out and he will return to his earth —
\q2 in that day all his thoughts will perish.
\q1
\v 5 Blessed is the one whose helper is the God of Jacob —
\q2 whose hope is upon the Lord his God —
\q1
\v 6 who made heaven and earth —
\q2 the sea and all that is in them —
\q1 who keeps truth to the age —
\q2
\v 7 who executes judgment for those who are wronged —
\q1 who gives food to the hungry.
\q1 The Lord loosens those in bonds —
\q2
\v 8 the Lord gives wisdom to the blind —
\q1 the Lord raises up those who are bowed down —
\q2 the Lord loves the righteous —
\q1
\v 9 the Lord guards the sojourners —
\q2 orphan and widow he will take up —
\q1 and the way of sinners he will destroy.
\q1
\v 10 The Lord will reign to the age —
\q2 your God O Zion to generation and generation.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 146
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Praise the Lord —
\q2 for a psalm is good —
\q1 to our God — pleasant is praise.
\q1
\v 2 The Lord builds up Jerusalem —
\q2 and will gather the scattered ones of Israel.
\q1
\v 3 Who heals the broken of heart —
\q2 and binds up their wounds.
\q1
\v 4 Who counts the multitude of stars —
\q2 and calls them all by name.
\q1
\v 5 Great is our Lord and great is his power —
\q2 and of his understanding there is no number.
\q1
\v 6 The Lord takes up the gentle —
\q2 but humbles sinners to the ground.
\q1
\v 7 Begin to the Lord with confession —
\q2 make a psalm to our God on the harp.
\q1
\v 8 Who covers the heaven with clouds —
\q2 who prepares rain for the earth —
\q1 who makes grass spring up on the mountains —
\q2 and green herb for the service of men.
\q1
\v 9 Who gives to the cattle their food —
\q2 and to the young of ravens who call upon him.
\q1
\v 10 He will not take pleasure in the strength of a horse —
\q2 nor in the legs of a man will he take pleasure.
\q1
\v 11 The Lord takes pleasure in those who fear him —
\q2 and in those who hope in his mercy.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 147
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Praise the Lord O Jerusalem —
\q2 praise your God O Zion.
\q1
\v 2 For he strengthened the bars of your gates —
\q2 he blessed your sons within you.
\q1
\v 3 Who sets peace on your borders —
\q2 and fills you with the fat of wheat.
\q1
\v 4 Who sends his word to the earth —
\q2 his word runs quickly.
\q1
\v 5 Who gives snow as wool —
\q2 he scatters mist as ashes.
\q1
\v 6 Who casts his ice as bread-crumbs —
\q2 before his cold — who will stand?
\q1
\v 7 He will send his word and melt them —
\q2 his breath-of-life will blow and waters will flow.
\q1
\v 8 Who announces his word to Jacob —
\q2 his ordinances and his judgments to Israel.
\q1
\v 9 He did not do so to every nation —
\q2 and his judgments he did not make known to them.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 148
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Praise the Lord from the heavens —
\q2 praise him in the heights.
\q1
\v 2 Praise him all his angels —
\q2 praise him all his hosts.
\q1
\v 3 Praise him sun and moon —
\q2 praise him all the stars and light.
\q1
\v 4 Praise him the heaven of heavens —
\q2 and the water above the heavens.
\q1
\v 5 Let them praise the name of the Lord —
\q2 for he himself spoke and they came to be —
\q1 he himself commanded and they were created.
\q1
\v 6 He established them to the age and to the age of ages —
\q2 he set an ordinance and it will not pass away.
\q1
\v 7 Praise the Lord from the earth —
\q2 dragons and all the depths —
\q1
\v 8 fire — hail — snow — ice —
\q2 breath-of-life of the storm —
\q1 which does his word —
\q2
\v 9 mountains and all the hills —
\q1 fruit trees and all the cedars —
\q2
\v 10 wild animals and all the cattle —
\q1 creeping things and winged birds —
\q2
\v 11 kings of the earth and all peoples —
\q1 rulers and all judges of the earth —
\q2
\v 12 young men and virgins —
\q1 old men with youths —
\q2
\v 13 let them praise the name of the Lord —
\q1 for his name alone is exalted.
\q1 His acknowledgment is over earth and heaven —
\q2
\v 14 and he will exalt the horn of his people.
\q1 A hymn for all his holy ones —
\q2 for the sons of Israel — a people drawing near to him.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 149
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Sing to the Lord a new song —
\q2 his praise in the gathering of holy ones.
\q1
\v 2 Let Israel be glad in the one who made him —
\q2 and let the sons of Zion rejoice in their king.
\q1
\v 3 Let them praise his name in a dance —
\q2 on timbrel and harp let them make a psalm to him.
\q1
\v 4 For the Lord takes pleasure in his people —
\q2 and will exalt the gentle with salvation.
\q1
\v 5 The holy ones will boast in glory —
\q2 and will rejoice upon their beds.
\q1
\v 6 The high praises of God in their throat —
\q2 and two-edged swords in their hand —
\q1
\v 7 to execute vengeance among the nations —
\q2 rebukes among the peoples —
\q1
\v 8 to bind their kings in fetters —
\q2 and their honored ones in iron manacles —
\q1
\v 9 to execute in them the written judgment —
\q2 this is glory for all his holy ones.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 150
\d Hallelujah.
\q1
\v 1 Praise God in his holy ones —
\q2 praise him in the firmament of his power.
\q1
\v 2 Praise him for his mighty acts —
\q2 praise him according to the multitude of his greatness.
\q1
\v 3 Praise him with the sound of a trumpet —
\q2 praise him with psaltery and harp.
\q1
\v 4 Praise him with timbrel and dance —
\q2 praise him with strings and flute.
\q1
\v 5 Praise him with melodious cymbals —
\q2 praise him with cymbals of shouting.
\q1
\v 6 Let every breath-of-life praise the Lord.
\p Hallelujah.

\c 151
\d This psalm is a genuine one of David — though outside the number — when he fought in single combat with Goliath.
\q1
\v 1 I was small among my brothers —
\q2 and the youngest in my father's house —
\q1 I tended my father's sheep.
\q1
\v 2 My hands made a musical instrument —
\q2 and my fingers tuned a psaltery.
\q1
\v 3 And who will announce to my Lord?
\q2 The Lord himself — he himself hears.
\q1
\v 4 He himself sent his messenger —
\q2 and took me from my father's sheep —
\q1 and anointed me with the oil of his anointing.
\q1
\v 5 My brothers were fair and tall —
\q2 but the Lord did not take pleasure in them.
\q1
\v 6 I went out to meet the Philistine —
\q2 and he cursed me by his idols.
\q1
\v 7 But I drew his own sword from him —
\q2 and beheaded him —
\q1 and took away reproach from the sons of Israel.

# PSALMS BOOK 1 — GLE v2.1 Notes
## LXX Psalms 1–40 (MT Psalms 1–41)

**Text base:** Rahlfs-Hanhart LXX Greek  
**Engine:** Thayer (1889) primary anchor | LSJ Septuagintal depth layer | Vulgate friction witness  
**CAE contamination block:** Active throughout  
**Canon:** Greek-Catholic Psalter | LXX numbering throughout

---

## STRUCTURAL NOTE — LXX BOOK 1

Book 1 closes at LXX Psalm 40 with the doxology: "Blessed is the Lord the God of Israel — from the age and to the age. Amen — Amen." The double Amen is the editorial seam-marker across all five books. The LXX Book 1 = MT Psalms 1–41 — the correspondence is exact here since the LXX/MT numbering divergence has not yet begun (it begins at LXX Psalm 9 which combines MT 9+10, but the book division falls after the rejoined sequence so Book 1 ends at the same point in both traditions).

---

## CAE NODES — BOOK 1

### Psalm 1 — *makarios* / blessed — the beatitude-formula opener

The Psalter opens with *makarios* = blessed — the same vocabulary as the Beatitudes of Matthew 5:3–12. *Makarios* = in a state of divine favor, happy in the deepest sense — from *makar* = blessed one. The Psalter's opening word sets the whole collection under the beatitude-register. The engine rendered "blessed" — not "happy" — preserving the divine-favor register throughout Book 1.

***anomous* / *hamartōlous* / *loimōn*** — three parallel negative figures in Psalm 1:1 — the ungodly / sinners / the pestilent. *Anomos* = lawless one (without *nomos* = law). *Hamartōlos* = sinner (from *hamartanō* = to miss the mark). *Loimos* = pestilent one, a plague-carrier — LSJ: a pest, a scoundrel. The three-fold descent from ungodly to sinner to pestilent marks the progressive alignment with evil the blessed man avoids. The engine preserved all three distinct terms.

***meletaō*** — "in his law he meditates day and night" — *meletaō* = to practice, to exercise, to meditate upon. LSJ: to care for, to practice assiduously. Not passive contemplation but active, repeated engagement — the same word used for an orator practicing a speech. The engine rendered "meditates" — the continual active return to the law.

---

### Psalm 2 — *huios mou ei sy* / you are my son — CAE PRIMARY NODE

**2:7** — "The Lord said to me: You are my son — today I have begotten you."

***huios mou ei sy*** = you are my son. *Egennēsa se* = I have begotten you — *gennaō* = to beget, the generative act. The "today" (*sēmeron*) of the begetting is the exegetical crux: the psalm's royal coronation context uses begetting-language for the king's enthronement.

**Acts 13:33** — Paul at Pisidian Antioch applies this to the resurrection as the day of begetting — the enthronement of the risen Son. **Hebrews 1:5** — the Son's superiority to angels grounded in this address. **Hebrews 5:5** — the high priestly appointment grounded in the sonship-declaration.

The engine rendered "today I have begotten you" — preserving the *gennaō* begetting-vocabulary the NT citations require.

***christos*** = the anointed one — "against the Lord and against his anointed." Acts 4:25–26 quotes Psalm 2:1–2 as fulfilled in the gathering against the Lord's anointed. The engine rendered "anointed" — keeping the LXX Greek register.

***poimaneis autous en rhabdō sidērā*** = you will shepherd them with a rod of iron — Revelation 2:27, 12:5, and 19:15 all cite this. The engine rendered "shepherd" for *poimainō* — not "rule" — preserving the shepherd-staff register.

---

### Psalm 8 — *hyios anthrōpou* / son of man — CAE NODE

**8:4–6** — "What is man that you remember him — or the son of man that you visit him? You made him a little less than angels — you crowned him with glory and honor — you subjected all things under his feet."

**Hebrews 2:6–8** quotes Psalm 8:4–6 extensively — Jesus as the true Son of Man who fulfills what Adam/humanity failed to do. The "little less than angels" receives the Hebrews reading as "for a little while less than angels" in the incarnation. **1 Corinthians 15:27** also cites "you subjected all things under his feet" in the resurrection-reign argument.

***elattōsas auton brachy ti par' angelous*** = you made him a little less than angels — the LXX renders Hebrew *'Elohim* as *angelous* = angels, which becomes the basis for Hebrews 2's argument. The engine rendered "a little less than angels" — the LXX reading.

---

### Psalm 15 (LXX) — *ouk egkataleiepseis tēn psychēn mou eis hadēn* — CAE NODE

**15:10** — "For you will not leave my soul in Hades — nor will you give your holy one to see corruption."

**Acts 2:27** — Peter at Pentecost quotes this verbatim as prophecy of the resurrection: David died and saw corruption — therefore David prophesied of another. **Acts 13:35** — Paul quotes it again with the same argument.

***hosiou*** = your holy one — *hosios* = the devout/pious/holy one. The engine rendered "your holy one" — the messianic designation.

**15:8** — "I set the Lord always before me — for he is at my right hand so that I may not be shaken." The *ek dexiōn mou* = at my right hand — the right-hand vocabulary connecting to Psalm 110:1.

---

### Psalm 17 (LXX) — The Great Davidic Psalm

***agapēsō se kyrie ischys mou*** = "I will love you O Lord my strength" (17:1) — *agapaō* opening the declaration of love — the Shema's love-command enacted in praise.

***ōdines hadou*** = "pangs of Hades" (17:5) — *ōdin* = birth-pang. Acts 2:24 — "God raised him up having loosed the pangs of death (*tas ōdinas tou thanatou*)" — fires on this exact vocabulary.

**17:49** — "Therefore I will confess to you among the nations O Lord — and I will sing a psalm to your name." Romans 15:9 quotes this in Paul's catena of OT texts supporting Gentile inclusion in worship.

---

### Psalm 21 (LXX) — The Passion Psalm — CAE PRIMARY NODE

LXX Psalm 21 = MT Psalm 22. The NT citation density of this psalm is unmatched in the entire Psalter.

**21:1** — "O God my God — attend to me — why have you forsaken me?" Matthew 27:46 and Mark 15:34 quote the cry from the cross — Psalm 21 is the psalm Jesus prays at the crucifixion.

**21:8** — "All who saw me mocked me — they spoke with their lips — they shook their heads." Matthew 27:39 — those passing by shaking their heads — direct citation.

**21:9** — "He hoped in the Lord — let him rescue him — let him save him for he delights in him." Matthew 27:43 — the mockers at the cross quote this directly.

**21:16** — "They pierced my hands and my feet." The LXX reads *ōryxan* = they dug/pierced — the piercing-reading that forms the basis for the NT passion typology.

**21:18** — "They divided my garments among themselves — and over my clothing they cast a lot." John 19:24 quotes this verbatim as fulfilled at the cross — one of the most explicit passion-fulfillment citations in the gospels. The engine preserved "garments" (*himatia*, plural) and "clothing" (*himatismon*, singular) as distinct — tracking John's two-garment distinction.

**21:22** — "I will recount your name to my brothers — in the midst of the gathering I will praise you." Hebrews 2:12 quotes this as the risen Christ announcing the Father's name to his brothers.

---

### Psalm 22 (LXX) — *kyrios poimainei me* / the Lord shepherds me

***skiā thanatou*** = shadow of death — the LXX compound rendered as "shadow of death" — not "deep darkness." The engine followed the LXX reading.

***to potērion sou*** = your cup — the *potērion* = cup vocabulary carries Eucharistic resonance (1 Corinthians 10:21 — *potērion kyriou* = the cup of the Lord).

---

### Psalm 23 (LXX) — *basileus tēs doxēs* / King of glory

The superscription "for the first day of the week" is a LXX liturgical addition — preserved by the engine. The everlasting-doors entry of the King of glory fires in ascension and enthronement typology throughout the NT.

---

### Psalm 30 (LXX) — *eis cheiras sou parathēsomai to pneuma mou* — CAE NODE

**30:5** — "Into your hands I will commit my breath-of-life — you ransomed-released me O Lord God of truth."

**Luke 23:46** — the dying words of Jesus from the cross are Psalm 30:5 LXX quoted verbatim: "Father into your hands I commit my breath-of-life." The engine rendered "breath-of-life" — not "spirit" — preserving the *pneuma* chain and the Luke 23:46 citation vocabulary.

---

### Psalm 31 (LXX) — *makarios hōn aphethēsan* — CAE NODE

**31:1–2** — "Blessed are those whose lawless deeds were forgiven — and whose sins were covered. Blessed is the man to whom the Lord will not reckon sin."

**Romans 4:7–8** quotes Psalm 31:1–2 as OT ground for imputed righteousness. The *logizomai* = reckon/count vocabulary connects Genesis 15:6 (Abraham) and Psalm 31 in Paul's argument. The engine rendered "reckon" for *logizomai* — the accounting-register vocabulary.

---

### Psalm 33 (LXX) — *geuasthe kai idete* / taste and see

**33:8** — "Taste and see that the Lord is good." 1 Peter 2:3 cites this as the experience of newborn believers longing for the word.

**33:20** — "He guards all his bones — not one of them will be broken." John 19:36 — "so that the scripture might be fulfilled: Not a bone of his will be broken." The bone-preservation of the righteous in Psalm 33:20 becomes the passion-fulfillment citation for Jesus' unbroken legs at the cross. The engine preserved "not one of them will be broken" at full precision.

---

### Psalm 39 (LXX) — *ōta de katērtisō moi* / ears you prepared for me — LXX/Hebrews divergence

**39:6** — "Sacrifice and offering you did not desire — but ears you prepared for me."

***ōta de katērtisō moi*** = but ears you prepared for me — the Rahlfs-Hanhart LXX reading. The MT reads "ears you opened/dug for me."

**Hebrews 10:5** cites this with *sōma de katērtisō moi* = "but a body you prepared for me" — a LXX text-form variant not in Rahlfs-Hanhart. The Hebrews application is to the incarnation: the body prepared for Christ as the vehicle of the once-for-all sacrifice.

**Engine decision:** Render the Rahlfs-Hanhart LXX text (*ōta* = ears). The Hebrews 10:5 variant is a notes-level observation — not a text-level correction.

**39:7** — "Look I come — in the heading of the scroll it is written concerning me." Hebrews 10:7 quotes this as the Son's words at the incarnation. The engine rendered "heading of the scroll" for *kephalidi bibliou* — *kephalís* = the knob at the top of a scroll-roller, hence the scroll's heading/beginning.

---

### Psalm 40 (LXX) — *ho trōgōn artous mou* / who ate my bread — CAE NODE

**40:9** — "For even the man of my peace in whom I trusted — who ate my bread — lifted up his heel against me."

**John 13:18** — Jesus at the Last Supper cites this as fulfilled in Judas's betrayal: "The one eating my bread lifted up his heel against me." The *trōgōn* = eating verb is the same used in John 6:54–58 for eating the flesh of the Son of Man — the Eucharistic register is present. The engine rendered "who ate my bread" preserving *trōgōn*.

---

## VOCABULARY DECISIONS — BOOK 1 SYSTEMATIC

**Hades** — rendered as "Hades" throughout (Psalms 6:5, 9:17, 15:10, 17:5, 29:3, 30:17) — never "hell" or "grave."

**Pneuma** — rendered as "breath-of-life" in all occurrences — both divine breath (32:6 — "by the breath-of-life of his mouth") and human breath-of-life (30:5 — "I will commit my breath-of-life").

**Dikaiosynē** — rendered as "righteousness" throughout — not "justice" (which is *krisis* / *krima*).

**Eleos** — rendered as "mercy" throughout — the LXX rendering of Hebrew *hesed* = covenant-faithfulness. Not "lovingkindness" or "love."

**Doxa** — rendered as "glory" throughout — not "honor" (*timē*) or "splendor."

**Exomologeomai** — rendered as "confess" in sin-confession register (31:5) and "confess to" in praise-thanksgiving register (17:49, 29:12) — the dative construction distinguishes the two.

**Makarios** — rendered as "blessed" throughout — not "happy."

---

## SUPERSCRIPTION VOCABULARY

***eis to telos*** = for the end — *telos* = end, completion, goal — rendered "for the end" throughout.

***diapsalma*** = preserved untranslated in text as `diapsalma` — the LXX rendering of *selah*.

---

## DOXOLOGY — CLOSE OF BOOK 1

**Psalm 40:13** — "Blessed is the Lord the God of Israel — from the age and to the age. Amen — Amen."

*Eulogētos* = blessed (of God) — the benediction-formula applied upward. *Apo tou aiōnos kai eis ton aiōna* = from the age and to the age. Double *amēn amēn* = congregational ratification preserved.

---
